בְּשׁוּבְעִין נַפְשָׁתָא נְחָתוּ אַבְהַתְכוֹן לְמִצְרַיִם וּכְדוֹן שַׁוִינְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן הֵי כְּכוֹכְבֵי שְׁמַיָא לְמִסְגֵי


With seventy souls your fathers went down into Mizraim, and now hath the Lord your God set you as the stars of the heavens for multitude.



## Chapter 11

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:1)


וּתְרַחֲמוּן יַת יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וְתִינְטְרוּן מַטְרַת מֵימְרֵיהּ וּקְיָימוֹי וְדִינוֹי וּפִקּוּדוֹי כָּל יוֹמַיָא


Therefore shall you love the Lord your God, and diligently observe His Word, His statutes, and His judgments always.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:2)


וְתִנְדְּעוּן יוֹמָא דֵין אֲרוּם לָא עִם בְּנֵיכוֹן דְּלָא יָדְעוּן וְלָא חָמוּן יַת אוּלְפַן אוֹרַיְיתָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יַת רְבוּתֵיהּ וְיַת יְדֵיהּ תַקִיפְתָּא וּדְרָעֵיהּ מְרַמַם


And know you this day, for (I speak) not with your children who have not known or seen the instruction of the law of the Lord your God, nor His greatness, nor His mighty hand, nor His uplifted arm,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:3)


וְיַת אַתְוָותֵיהּ וְיַת עוֹבָדוֹי דְעָבַד בְּגוֹ מִצְרַיִם לְפַרְעה מַלְכָּא דְמִצְרַיִם וּלְכָל יַתְבֵי אַרְעֵיהּ


or His signs and works which He wrought in Mizraim, on Pharoh king of Mizraim, and on all the inhabitants of his land;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:4)


וּדְעָבַד לְמַשִׁרְיַית מִצְרַיִם לְסוּסְוַותְהוֹן וּלְאַרְתִיכֵיהוֹן דִּי אַטִּיף יַת מוֹי דְיַמָא דְסוּף עַל אַפֵּיהוֹן כַּד רְדָפוּ בַּתְרֵיכוֹן וְאַבֵּידִינוּן יְיָ עַד זְמַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין


what He did also to the hosts of Mizraim, to their horses and chariots, when He made the waters of the Red Sea to overwhelm their faces when they followed after you, when the Lord destroyed them unto this day’s time;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:5)

and what He hath done to you in the wilderness till the time that you came to this place;

for with your eyes have you seen all the great work of the Lord which He hath wrought.

Therefore shall you keep all the precepts which I command you this day, that you may be strengthened, and go in, and inherit the land to possess which you go over;

וּמִן בִּגְלַל דְּיִסְגוּן יוֹמֵיכוֹן עַל אַרְעָא דְקַיֵּים יְיָ לְאַבְהַתְכוֹן לְמִתַּן לְהוֹן וְלִבְנֵיהוֹן אַרְעָא דְפֵירָהָא שְׁמֵינִין כַּחֲלָב וְחַלְיַין כִּדְבָשׁ


and that your days may be multiplied upon the land which the Lord sware to your fathers to give it to them and their children; a land whose fruits are rich as milk, and sweet as honey.

For the land to which thou goest in to possess it is not like the land of Mizraim, from whence you have come, in which thou didst sow thy seed, and water it thyself as a garden of herbs;

And it shall be that if you diligently obey My commandments which I command you this day, to love the Lord your God, and to serve Him with all your heart, and with all your soul,

and the Lord’s anger be provoked against you, and He shut up the clouds of heaven, and let not the rain come down, and the earth yield no provender, and you perish soon from off the glorious land which the Lord shall give you.

But lay these my words upon your heart, and upon your soul, and bind them, written upon tephillin, as a sign upon the upper part (wrist?) of your left hands, and let them be for tephillin over thy forehead between thy eyes.

And thou shalt teach them to thy children to study them when you are sitting in your house with your kindred, and when you are walking in the way, and in the evening when you lie down, and in the morning when you arise.

וְתִכְתְּבִינוּן בִּמְגִילְתָּא עַל מְזוּזְיַין וְתִקְבְּעִינוּן בְּתוֹלְתָּא קֳבֵל תִּקֵי בְּסִיפֵי בָתֵּיכוֹן וּבְתַרְעֵיכוֹן


And you shall write them upon parchment, upon the posts, and affix them to three (things), against thy chest, against the pillars of thy house, and against thy gates:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:21)


מִן בִּגְלַל דְּיִסְגוּן יוֹמֵיכוֹן וְיוֹמֵי בְּנֵיכוֹן עַל אַרְעָא דְקַיֵּים יְיָ לְאַבְהַתְכוֹן לְמִיתְנָא לְהוֹן כִּסְכוֹם יוֹמִין דְּקַיְימִין שְׁמַיָא עַל אַרְעָא


that your days and the days of your children may be multiplied on the land which the Lord sware to your fathers to give you, as the number of the days that the heavens abide over the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:22)


אֲרוּם אִין מִנְטוֹר תִּנְטְרוּן יַת כָּל תַּפְקֵידְתָּא הֲדָא דַאֲנָא מְפַקֵּיד יַתְכוֹן לְמַעַבְדָהּ לְמִרְחַם יַת יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לִמְהַךְ בְּכָל אָרְחָן דְּתַקְנָן קֳדָמוֹי וּמְטוּל לְאִתְקְרָבָא לִדְחַלְתֵּיהּ


For if you diligently keep every commandment that I command you to do it, to love the Lord your God, and walk in all the ways that are right before Him, and cleave unto His fear,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:23)


וִיתָרִיךְ מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ יַת כָּל עַמְמַיָא הָאִלֵּין מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְתֵירְתוּן עַמְמִין רַבְרְבִין וְתַקִּיפִין מִנְכוֹן


then will the Word of the Lord drive out all these nations from before you, and you shall possess the heritage of nations greater and stronger than yourselves.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:24)


כָּל אַתְרָא דִי תִדְרוֹךְ פַּרְסַת רִגְלֵיכוֹן בֵּיהּ דִּילְכוֹן יְהֵי מִן מַדְבְּרָא וְטַוַור לְכוֹן הִינוּן טַוְורֵי בֵּית מַקְדְּשָׁא מִן נַהֲרָא רַבָּא נַהֲרָא פְּרָת עַד יַמָא דְאוֹקְיָנוּס הִינוּן מֵי בְרֵאשִׁית סְטַר מַעַרְבָא יְהֵי תְחוּמֵיכוֹן


Every place where the sole of your foot shall tread will be yours, from the wilderness and the mountain; (among) your mountains shall be the house of the sanctuary, and from the great river, the River Phrat, unto the ocean sea, whose waters are (old as) the creation, on the western side shall be your limit.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:25)


לָא יִתְעַתֵּד בַּר נַשׁ בְּאַפֵּיכוֹן דַּחֲלַתְכוֹן וְאֵימַתְכוֹן יִתֵּן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן עַל אַפֵּי כָּל יַתְבֵי אַרְעָא דְּתִדְרְכוּן בָּהּ הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵּיל לְכוֹן


Not a man will be able to stand before you; but the Lord your God will set the fear and dread of you upon the faces of all the inhabiters of the land that you tread upon, as it hath been told you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:26)


ראה אנכי אָמַר משֶׁה נְבִיָא חֲמוֹן דַּאֲנָא מְסַדֵּר קֳדָמֵיכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין בִּרְכָתָא וְחִילוּפָהּ


MOSHEH the prophet said: Behold, I have this day set in order before you a Blessing and its contrary:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:27)


יַת בִּרְכָתָא אִין תְּקַבְּלוּן לְפִיקוּדַיָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דַּאֲנָא מְפַקֵּיד לְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין


the Blessing, if you will be obedient to the commandments of the Lord your God which I command you this day;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:28)


וְחִילוּפָא אִין לָא תְקַבְּלוּן לְפִיקוּדַיָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וְתִטְעוּן מִן אָרְחָא דַאֲנָא מְפַקֵיד יַתְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין לְמִטְעֵי בָּתַר טַעֲוַות עַמְמַיָא דְלָא יְדַעְתּוּן


and its contrary, if you will not obey the commandments of the Lord your God, but will go astray from the path which I have taught you this day, in turning aside after the idols of the nations whom thou hast not known.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:29)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם יֵיעָל יַתְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְאַרְעָא דְאַתּוּן עָלְלִין לְתַמָּן לְמֵירְתָהּ וְתִתְּנוּן שִׁית שִׁבְטִין עַל טַוְורָא דִגְרִיזִים וְשִׁית שִׁבְטִין עַל טַוְורָא דְעֵיבָל מְבָרְכַיָא יֶהֱווֹן הָפְכִין אַפֵּיהוֹן לְקָבֵיל טַוְורָא דִגְרִיזִים וּמְלַטְטַיָא יֶהֱווֹן הָפְכִין אַפֵּיהוֹן לְקָבֵל טַוְורָא דְעֵיבָל


And it shall be, when the Lord your God will have brought you to the land into which you are going, to possess it, you shall place six tribes upon the mountain of Gerizim, and six tribes on the mountain of Ebal. They who recite the blessings shall turn their faces towards Mount Gerizim, and they who recite the curses shall turn their faces towards Mount Ebal.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:30)


הֲלָא אִינוּן יְהִיבִין מִלְהָלָא לְיוֹרְדְּנָא אֲחוֹרֵי אוֹרַח מַטְמְעָא דְשִׁמְשָׁא בְּאַרַע כְּנַעֲנָאָה דְשָׁרֵי בְּמֵישְׁרָא כֻּלּוֹ קֳבֵיל גֻּלְגְּלָא בְּסִטְרֵי חֶזְוֵי מַמְרֵא


Are they not situated beyond Jordan by the way of the sunset, in the land of the Kenaanah, who dwell in the plain over against Gilgela by the side (of the place) of the vision of Mamre?

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:31)


אֲרוּם אַתּוּן עָבְרִין יַת יוֹרְדְּנָא לְמֵיעַל לְמֵירוֹת יַת אַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן וְתֵירְתוּן יָתָהּ וְתֵיתְבוּן בָּהּ


For you are to pass over Jordan to enter and possess the land which the Lord your God giveth you, and you will hold and will dwell therein.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/11:32)


וְתִטְרוּן לְמֶעֱבַד יַת כָּל קְיָימַיָא וְיַת דִּינַיָא דִי אֲנָא יָהֵיב קֳדָמֵיכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין


Look well, therefore, that you perform all the statutes and judgments that I have set before you this day.



## Chapter 12

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:1)


אִלֵין קְיָימַיָא וְדִינַיָיא דְתִנְטְרוּן לְמֶעֱבַד בְּאַרְעָא דִיהַב יְיָ אֱלָהָא דְאַבְהַתְכוֹן לְכוֹן לְמֵירְתָהּ כָּל יוֹמַיָא דְאַתּוּן קְיָימִין עַל אַרְעָא


These are the statutes and judgments which you are to observe to do in the land which the Lord God of your fathers giveth you to inherit all the days that you live upon the earth.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:2)


אַבָּדָא תְאַבְּדוּן יַת כָּל אַתְרַיָא דִי פְלָחוּ תַּמָּן עַמְמַיָא דְאַתּוּן יַרְתוּן יַתְהוֹן וְיַת טַעֲוַותְהוֹן עַל טַוְורַיָא רָמַיָא וְעַל גְּלִימָתָא וּתְחוֹת כָּל אִילַן דְּרִיוְיֵיהּ שַׁפִּיר


You shall utterly destroy all the places in which the people (whose land) you will possess have worshipped their idols, upon the high mountains and hills, and under every tree of beautiful form.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:3)


וְתִסְתְּרוּן יַת אֲגוּרֵיהוֹן וְתִתְבְּרוּן יַת קָמַתְהוֹן וּמְרַחֲקַתְהוֹן תּוֹקְדוּן בְּנוּרָא וְצִילְמֵי טַעֲוַותְהוֹן תְּשַׁקְצוּן וּתְשֵׁיצִינוּן יַת שִׁמְהוֹן מִן אַתְרָא הַהוּא


You shall lay their altars in ruin, break down their pillars, burn their abominations with fire, and utterly destroy the images of their gods, and abolish their names from that place.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:4)


לֵיתֵיכוֹן רְשָׁאִין לְמַחְקָא כְּתַב שְׁמָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


Not so may you do to blot out the inscription of the Name of the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:5)


אֱלָהֵן בְּאַרְעָא דְיִתְרְעֵי מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מִן כָּל שִׁבְטֵיכוֹן לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן לְבֵית שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תִּתְבְּעוּן וְתֵיתוּן תַּמָּן


But in the land which the Word of the Lord your God will choose out of all your tribes for His Shekinah to dwell there, unto the place of His Shekinah shall you have recourse, and come thither,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:6)


וְתַיְיתוּן תַּמָּן עַלְוַותְכוֹן וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיכוֹן וְיַת מַעֲשַרְתֵּיכוֹן וְיַת אַפְרָשׁוּת יְדֵיכוֹן וְנִדְרֵיכוֹן וְנִיסְבָתֵיכוֹן וּבְכוֹרֵי תוֹרֵיכוֹן וְעָנֵיכוֹן


and bring your sacrifices and consecrated oblations, your tythes, the separation of your hands, your vows, your voluntary offerings, and the firstlings of your herds and flocks.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:7)


וְתֵיכְלוּן תַּמָּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וְתֵחְדוּן בְּכָל אוֹשָׁטוּת יֶדְכוֹן אַתּוּן וְאֵינַשׁ בָּתֵּיכוֹן דִּי בְּרַכְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


And you shall there eat before the Lord your God, and rejoice in all that you put your hand unto, you and your households, in which the Lord your God will have blessed you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:8)


לֵיתֵיכוֹן רַשָּׁאִין לְמֶעֱבַד הֵיכְמָא דַאֲנָן עָבְדִין הָכָא יוֹמָא דֵין גְּבַר כָּל דְּכָשַׁר בְּעֵינוֹי


It will not be lawful for you to do (there) as we do here today, whatever any one thinks fit for himself;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:9)


אֲרוּם לָא אָתֵיתוּן עַד כְּדוֹן לְבֵי מוּקְדְּשָׁא דְהוּא בֵּית נְיָיחָא וּלְאַחְסָנַת אַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן


for you are not yet come to the Sanctuary, to the dwelling of Peace, and to the inheritance of the land which the Lord your God will give you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:10)


וְתַעַבְרוּן יַת יוֹרְדְּנָא וְתֵיתְבוּן בְּאַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מַחְסַן יַתְכוֹן וִינִיחַ לְכוֹן מִכָּל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן מִן חֲזוֹר חֲזוֹר וְתִבְנוּן בֵּית מוּקְדְּשָׁא וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין תֵּיתְבוּן לְרוֹחֲצָן


But when you have passed over Jordan and dwell in the land which the Lord your God will give you to inherit, and He hath given you repose from all your enemies round about, then shall you build the house of the Sanctuary, and afterward shall dwell securely.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:11)


וִיהֵי אַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי בֵּיהּ מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן לְתַמָּן תַּיְיתוּן יַת כָּל קוּרְבָּנַיָא וּבִכּוּרַיָא וּמַעַשְרַיָא דַאֲנָא מְפַקֵּיד יַתְכוֹן תַּמָּן תִּקְרְבוּן עֲלָוַותְכוֹן וְנִכְסַת קוּדְשֵׁיכוֹן וְתַמָּן תֵּיכְלוּן מַעַשְרֵיכוֹן וְאַפְרָשׁוּת יְדֵיכוֹן וְכָל שְׁפַר נִדְרֵיכוֹן דְּתִידְרוּן קֳדָם יְיָ


And to the place which the Word of the Lord will choose to make His Shekinah to dwell there, shall you bring all your oblations, firstlings, and tythes, which I command you; there shall you offer your sacrifices and hallowed victims, there eat your tythes and the separation of your hands, and all your goodly vows which you may have vowed before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:12)


וְתֶחְדוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן אַתּוּן וּבְנֵיכוֹן וּבְנָתֵיכוֹן וְעַבְדֵיכוֹן וְאַמְהַתְכוֹן וְלֵוָאֵי דִבְקִירְוֵיכוֹן אֲרוּם לֵית לֵיהּ חוּלַק וְאַחֲסָנָא עִמְכוֹן


And you shall rejoice before the Lord your God, you and your sons and daughters, your servants and handmaids, and the Levite who is in your cities, for he hath no portion or inheritance with you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:13)


אִסְתַּמָּרוּ לְכוֹן דִּילְמָא תַסְקוּן עֲלָוַותְכוֹן בְּכָל אַתְרָא דְאַתּוּן חַמְיָין


Beware lest you offer your sacrifices in any place which thou mayest see;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:14)


אֱלָהֵן לְאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ בְּאַחֲסָנַת חַד מִן שִׁבְטֵיכוֹן תַּמָּן תִּסְקוּן עֲלָוַותְכוֹן וְתַמָּן תַּעַבְדוּן יַת כָּל מַה דַאֲנָא מְפַקֵּיד לְכוֹן


but in the Place which the Lord will choose in the inheritance of one of your tribes, there shall you offer your sacrifices and do whatever I command you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:15)


לְחוֹד בְּכָל רַעֲוַות נַפְשְׁכוֹן תִּיכְסוּן וְתֵיכְלוּן בִּישְרָא הֵי כְּבִרְכָתָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דִּיהַב לְכוֹן בְּכָל קִירְוֵיכוֹן דִּמְסָאֲבִין מִן לְמִיקְרַב לְקוּדְשַׁיָא וְדִדְכִין לְמִקְרַב לְקוּדְשַׁיָא כַּחֲדָא יֵיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ כְּבִישְרֵיהּ דְּטַבְיָא וּכְאַיְלָא


Nevertheless, after every wish of your soul, you may kill and eat flesh according to the blessing of the Lord your God, which He will give you in all your cities; they who are unclean so as not to be able to offer holy things, and they who are clean that they may offer holy things, may eat of it alike, as the flesh of the antelope or of the hart.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:16)


לְחוֹד בְּאַדְמָא הֲווֹ זְהִירִין עַל אַרְעָא תֵּישְׁדוּנֵיהּ הֵי כְּמוֹי


Only be careful to pour out the blood upon the ground like water.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:17)


לֵיתֵיכוֹן רַשָּׁאִין לְמֵיכוּל בְּקִירְוֵיכוֹן מַעַשְרֵי עִיבּוּרֵיכוֹן חַמְרֵיכוֹן וּמִשְׁחֵיכוֹן וּבִיכּוּרֵי תּוֹרֵיכוֹן וְעַנְכוֹן וְכָל נִדְרֵיכוֹן דְּתִדְרוּן וְנִסְבָתֵיכוֹן וְאַפְרָשׁוּת יְדֵיכוֹן


It will not be lawful for you to eat the tenths of your corn, or wine, or oil, or the firstlings of your herd or flock, nor any of the vows that you have vowed, or freewill offerings, or the separation of your hands in your cities;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:18)


אֱלָהֵן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן תֵּיכְלוּנֵיהּ בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בֵּיהּ אַתּוּן וּבְנֵיכוֹן וּבְנָתֵיכוֹן וְעַבְדֵיכוֹן וְאַמְהוֹתֵיכוֹן וְלֵיוָאֵי דִבְקִירְוֵיכוֹן וְתֶחְדוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּכָל אוֹשָׁטוּת יְדֵיכוֹן


but you shall eat it before the Lord your God, in the place which the Lord your God will choose; you, and your sons and daughters, and your handmaids, and the Levites who are in your cities; and you shall rejoice before the Lord your God, in all that thou puttest thine hand unto.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:19)


אִסְתַּמָּרוּ לְכוֹן דִּלְמָא תִּימְעָלוּן עַל לֵוָאֵי כָּל יוֹמֵיכוֹן דְּאַתּוּן שָׁרַן עַל אַרְעֲכוֹן


Beware that thou aggrieve not the Levite all your days in which you dwell in your land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:20)


אֲרוּם יַפְתֵּי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יַת תְּחוּמְכוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵּיל לְכוֹן וְתֵימַר אֵיכוֹל בִּישְרָא אֲרוּם תֵירוּג נַפְשָׁךְ לְמֵיכוֹל בִּישְרָא בְּכָל רְגוּג נַפְשָׁךְ תֵּיכוּל בִּישְרָא


When the Lord your God will have enlarged your border, as He hath said unto you; and thou sayest, I would eat flesh, because thy soul may desire to eat flesh, thou mayest eat flesh according to all thy desire.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:21)


אֲרוּם יִתְרְחַק מִנְכוֹן אַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן וְתִכְסוּן מִן תּוֹרֵיכוֹן וּמִן עָנֵיכוֹן דִּיהַב יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְכוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵּדִית יַתְכוֹן וְתֵיכְלוּן בְּקִירְוֵיכוֹן בְּכָל רִיגוֹג נַפְשֵׁיכוֹן


But if the place which the Lord your God will have chosen that His Shekinah may dwell there be too far off, then may you eat of your herds and flocks which the Lord your God shall give you, as I have com- manded you, in your cities you may eat, according to all the desire of your soul:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:22

בְּרַם הֵיכְמָא דְמִתְאָכֵל בִּישְרָא דְטַבְיָא וְאַיְלָא הֵיכְדֵין תֵּיכְלוּנֵיהּ דִּמְסָאַב לְמִקְרַב לְקוּדְשַׁיָא וְדִדְכֵי לְמִקְרַב לְקוּדְשָׁא כַּחֲדָא תֵּיכְלוּן


as the flesh of the antelope or hart so may you eat it; he who is unclean that he may not offer holy things, and he who is clean that he may offer them, may eat of it alike.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:23)


לְחוֹד אִיתוֹקְפוּן בְּיִצְרֵיכוֹן מְטוּל דְּלָא לְמֵיכוּל אִידְמָא אֲרוּם אִידְמָא הוּא קִיּוּם נַפְשָׁא וְלָא תֵיכְלוּן אִידְמָא דְבֵיהּ קִיּוּם נַפְשָׁא עִם בִּישְרָא


Only put a strong restraint upon your desires, that you eat no blood; for the blood is the subsistence of the life. You may not, with the flesh, eat blood, in which is the subsistence of life:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:24)


לָא תֵיכְלוּנֵיהּ עַל אַרְעָא תִשְׁדוּנֵיהּ הֵי כְמַיָּא


you shall not eat it, you shall pour it out upon the ground like water:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:25)


לָא תֵיכְלוּנֵיהּ מִן בִּגְלַל דְּיֵיטִיב לְכוֹן וְלִבְנֵיכוֹן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן אֲרוּם תַּעַבְדוּן דְּכָשַׁר קֳדָם יְיָ


eat it not, that it may be well with you, and with your children after you, while you do that which is right before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:26

Nevertheless, animals which are your consecrated tenths, and your votive offerings, you shall take and bring to the place which the Lord will choose;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:27)


וְתַעֲבֵיד כְּהִלְכָתָא עֲלָוָותָךְ בִּסְרָא וְאַדְמָא עַל מַדְבְּחָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וַאֲדַם שְׁאַר נִכְסַת קוּדְשָׁךְ יִשְׁתְּפֵךְ עַל מַדְבְּחָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ וּבִשְרָא אִתְכְּשָׁר לְמֵיכוֹל


and thou shalt do (with them) according to the rite of thy burnt offerings, (and offer) the flesh and the blood upon the altar of the Lord thy God: the blood of the rest of thy holy oblations shall be poured out at the altar of the Lord thy God, but of the flesh it is lawful to eat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:28)


טוּרוּ וְקַבִּילוּ יַת פִּתְגָמַיָא דַאֲנָא מְפַקֵּיד לְכוֹן מִן בִּגְלַל דְּיֵיטִיב לְכוֹן וְלִבְנֵיכוֹן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן עַד עַלְמָא אֲרוּם תַּעַבְדוּן דְּתַקִּין וּדְכָשַׁר קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


Observe and obey all these words that I command you, that it may be well with you and with your children after you for ever, while you do that which is good and right before the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:29)


אֲרוּם יְשֵׁיצֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יַת עַמְמַיָא דְאַתּוּן עַלְלִין לְתַמָּן לְתַרְכוּתְהוֹן מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן וְתֵירַת יַתְהוֹן וְתֵיתַב בְּאַרְעֲהוֹן


When the Lord your God shall have cut off the nations among whom you go, and have expelled them from before you, and you inherit and dwell in their land,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:30)


אִסְתַּמָּרוּ לְכוֹן דִּלְמָא תִיתּוֹקְלוּן בָּתַר טַעֲוַותְהוֹן מִן בָּתַר דְּיִשְׁתֵּיצוּן מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן אוֹ דִלְמָא תִּתְבְּעוּן לְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן לְמֵימָר הֵיכְדֵין פַּלְחִין עַמְמַיָא הָאִלֵּין לְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן וְנַעֲבֵיד כְּדֵין אוּף אֲנָן


beware that you stumble not after their idols when they shall have been destroyed before you, or lest you seek after their idols, saying, How did these peoples worship their gods, that we may worship as they did?


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 12:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/12:31)


לָא תַעַבְדוּן פּוּלְחָנָא כְּדֵין לַייָ אֱלָהָכוֹן אֲרוּם כָּל דִּמְרַחֵק יְיָ דְסָנֵי עָבְדִין לְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן אֲרוּם אוּף יַת בְּנֵיהוֹן וְיַת בְּנָתֵיהוֹן כַּפְתִין וּמוֹקְדִין בְּנוּרָא לְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן


So shall you not do in serving the Lord your God; for whatever is abominable and hateful to Him have they done to their idols; for even their sons and daughters they have bound and burned with fire unto their idols.



## Chapter 13

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/13:1)


יַת כָּל פִּתְגָמַיָא דַאֲנָא מְפַקֵּיד יַתְכוֹן יָתֵיהּ תִּטְרוּן לְמֶעֱבַד לָא תוֹסְפוּן עֲלוֹי וְלָא תִבְצְרוּן מִנֵּיהּ


Whatsoever I command you, that shall you observe to do; ye shall not add to it nor diminish from it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 13:2

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:6)


וְכָל בְּעִירָא דִסְדִיקָא טַלְפַיָיא וְלָהּ קַרְנִין וּסְדִיק סִדְקָא מַסְקָא פִּישְׁרָא בִּבְעִירָא יָתָהּ תֵּיכְלוּן


and every animal that hath the divided hoof, and horns, and that cleaveth the cleft, bringing up the cud among animals, that you may eat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:7)


בְּרַם יַת דֵּין לָא תֵיכְלוּן מִמַּסְקֵי פִּישְׁרָא וּמִסְדִיקֵי פַּרְסָתָא שְׁלִילָא דְּלֵיהּ תְּרֵין רֵישִׁין וְתַרְתֵּין שַׁדְרָאִין הוֹאִיל וְלֵית בְּזַיְנֵיהּ חָזֵי לְמִתְקַיְימָא יַת גְּמָלָא וְיַת אַרְנְבָא וְיַת טַפְּזָא אֲרוּם מַסְקֵי פִּישְׁרָא הִינוּן וּפַרְסַתְהוֹן לָא סְדִיקָן מְסָאֲבִין הִינוּן לְכוֹן


But of these you may not eat that bring up the cud, or of those who (only) have the hoof divided, the cast thing (embryo) which hath two heads or a double back, things which are not to be perpetuated in the same species (i.e. as a species); nor the camel, the hare, or the coney, because they chew the cud, but do not divide the hoof; they are unclean to you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:8)


וְיַת חֲזִירָא אֲרוּם סְדִיק פַּרְסָתָא הוּא וְלֵית כַּנְפֵיק בֵּיהּ דְּסָדִיק וְלָא פָּשַׁר מְסָאַב הוּא לְכוֹן מִבִּסְרֵיהוֹן לָא תֵיכְלוּן וּבִנְבֵילְתְּהוֹן לָא תְקַרְבוּן


The swine, because, though he hath the hoof divided, and there is none produced that like him divideth (the hoof), and yet cheweth not the cud, is unclean to you; of their flesh you shall not eat, nor touch their dead bodies.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:9)


לְחוֹד יַת דֵּין תֵּיכְלוּן מִן כָּל דִּבְמַיָא כָּל דִּילֵיהּ צִיצִין לְמִפְרַח וְחַרְסְפִיתִין עַל מוּשְׁכֵיהּ וְאִין נַתְרוֹן וְאִשְׁתְּיֵיר חַד תְּחוֹת לִיסָתֵיהּ וְחַד תְּחוֹת צִיצִיתֵיהּ וְחַד תְּחוֹת גּוֹנְבֵיהּ יָתֵיהּ תֵּיכְלוּן


But this you may eat, of all that are in the waters, whatever hath fins to move, as by flying, and scales upon its skin; and though (some of which) may fall away, yet if there remain on under its jaw, another under its fin, and another under its tail, that you may eat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:10)


וְכָל דְּלֵית לֵיהּ צִיצִין וְחַרְסְפִיתִין לָא תֵיכְלוּן מְסָאָב הוּא לְכוֹן


But whatever hath neither fins nor scales you may not eat; it is unclean to you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:11)


כָּל צִפַּר דְּכֵי דְאִית בֵּיהּ זְפַק וְקוּרְקְבָנֵיהּ קָלִיף וְאִית לֵיהּ צִבְעָא יְתֵירָא וְלָא דָרִיס תֵּיכְלוּן


Every bird which hath a vesicle or crop which may be picked away, and which (bird) is longer than a finger, and not of the rapacious kind, you may eat.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:12)


וְדֵין דְּלָא תֵיכְלוּן מִנְהוֹן נִישְׁרָא וְעוּזָא וּבַר גּוֹזָא


But these are they which you may not eat: the eagle, the ossifrage, the osprey,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:13)


וְדַיְיתָא חִיוָורָא וְאוּכְמָתָא דְהִיא אִיבוּ וְדַיְיתָא לִזְנָהּ


the daitha (lammer geyer?) white or black, which is a bird of prey, a kind of vulture.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:14

וְיַת כָּל בְּנֵי עוּרְבָא לִזְנֵיהוֹן


And every raven after his kind;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:15)


וְיַת בְּרַת נַעֲמֵיתָא וְיַת חֲטִיפִיתָא וְיַת צִפַּר שַׁחֲפָא וְיַת בַּר נָצְצָא לִזְנוֹי


and the owl, and nighthawk, and the cuckoo, and the falcon after his kind;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:16)


וְיַת קְפוּפָא וְיַת שַׁלִי נוּנָא מִן יַמָּא וְיַת צַדְיָא


the great owl, and the sea gull (catcher of fish from the sea), and the night owl,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:17)


וְקָקָא חִיוַרְתָּא וְאוּכְמְתָא וּשְׁרַקְרְקָא


and the cormorant white or black, and the pica,

וְדַיְיתָא חִוַורְתָּא וְאוּכְמְתָא לִזְנָהּ וְנַגַר טוּרָא וְעַרְפְּרָא


and the stork white or black after its kind, and the heathcock, and the bat,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:19)


וְכָל דִּיבְבֵי וְזִיבוֹרֵי וְזִיזֵי דִטְלוֹפְחֵי וּפוּלֵי דְפַרְשֵׁיהּ מִן אוֹכְלָא וּפַרְחִין הֵי כְעוֹפָא מְסָאֲבִין אִינוּן לְכוֹן לָא יִתְאַכְלוּן


and all flies (bees) and wasps, and all worms of vegetables and pulse, which come away from (materials of) food and fly as birds, are unclean to you, they may not be eaten;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:20)


כָּל גּוּבָא דַכְיָא תֵּיכְלוּן


but any clean beast {locust} you may eat.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:21)


לָא תֵיכְלוּן כָּל דְּמִיקַלְקְלָא בְּנִכְסָא לְגִיוּר עָרֵל דִּבְקִירְוֵיכוֹן תִּיתְּנוּנָהּ וְיֵכְלוּנָהּ אוֹ תַּזְבִּין לְבַר עַמְמִין אֲרוּם עַם קַדִּישׁ אַתּוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לֵית אַתּוּן רַשָּׁאִין לְמִיבַשְׁלָא כָּל דְּכֵן לְמֵיכוֹל בְּשַר בַּחֲלָב תַּרְוֵיהוֹן מְעַרְבִין בְּחָדָא


You shall not eat of anything that is unclean through the manner of its death; you may give it to the uncircumcised stranger who is in your cities to eat it, or sell it to a son of the Gentiles; for you are a holy people before the Lord your God. It shall not be lawful for you to boil, much less to eat, flesh with milk when both are mixed together.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:22)


הֲווֹ זְהִירִין לְעַשְרָא פֵּירֵיכוֹן מִן דְּאַתּוּן מַפְקִין וּכְנָשׁוּן מִן חַקְלָא כָּל שַׁתָּא וְשַׁתָּא וְלָא פֵּירֵי שַׁתָּא עַל פֵּירֵי שַׁתָּא אוֹחֲרֵי


Be mindful to tythe your fruitage of whatsoever cometh forth, and which you gather in from the field year by year; not giving the fruit of one year for the fruit of another.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:23)


וְתֵיכְלוּן מַעַשְרָא תִנְיָינָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן מַעֲשַר עִיבּוּרֵיכוֹן חַמְרֵיכוֹן וּמִשְׁחֲכוֹן וְכֵן בִּכּוּרֵי תוֹרֵיכוֹן וְעָנֵיכוֹן מִן בִּגְלַל דְּתֵילְפוּן לְמִדְחַל מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן כָּל יוֹמַיָא


And the second tythe you shall eat before the Lord your God in the place which He will choose to make His Shekinah to dwell there; the tenths of your corn, your vines, and your oil, and likewise the firstlings of your oxen and sheep, that you may learn to fear the Lord your God all the days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:24)


וַאֲרוּם יִסְגֵי מִנְכוֹן אוֹרְחָא אֲרוּם לָא תֵיכוּל לְמִיסוֹבְרָא יַת מַעַשְרָא אֲרוּם יִתְרְחַק מִנְכוֹן אַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן אֲרוּם יְבָרְכִינָךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ


And if the way be too great for you to be able to carry the tenth, because the place which the Lord thy God will choose for His Shekinah to dwell there is too distant from you, when the Lord thy God shall have blessed thee,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:25)


וּתְחַלֵּל בְּכַסְפָּא וִיהוֹן פְּרִיטֵי צְרִירִין בִּידָךְ וּתְהַךְ לְאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהֲכוֹן בֵּיהּ


then thou mayest make exchange for it into silver, and bind the sum in thy hand, and proceed to the place which the Lord thy God shall choose,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:26)


וְתִתֵּן כַּסְפָּא בְּכָל דְּיִתְרְעֵי נַפְשָׁךְ בְּתוֹרֵי וּבְעָנָא וּבַחֲמַר חֲדַת וְעַתִּיק וּבְכָל דִּתְשַׁיְילִינָךְ נַפְשָׁךְ וְתֵיכְלוּן תַּמָּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וְתֶחְדוּן אַתּוּן וֶאֱנַשׁ בָּתֵּיכוֹן


and give the silver for any thing that thy soul pleaseth, of oxen, sheep, wine new or old, or whatever thy soul desireth; and you shall eat there before the Lord your God and rejoice, you and the men of your house.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:27)


וְלֵיוָאָה דִבְקִרְוֵיכוֹן לָא תִשְׁבְּקוּנֵיהּ אֲרוּם לֵית לֵיהּ חוֹלַק וְאַחְסָנָא עִמְכוֹן


And the Levite who is in your cities forsake not, for he hath not a portion or a heritage with you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:28)


מִסּוֹף תְּלַת שְׁנִין תַּפְקוּן יַת כָּל מַעֲשַר עֲלַלְתְּכוֹן בְּשַׁתָּא הַהוּא וְתַצְנִיעוּן בְּקִרְוֵיכוֹן


At the end of three years you shall bring forth all the tenths of your produce for that year, and lay them up in your cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 14:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/14:29)


וְיֵיתֵי לֵיוָאָה אֲרוּם לֵית לֵיהּ חוּלַק וְאַחֲסָנָא עִמְכוֹן וְגִיּוֹרָא וְיַתְמָא וְאַרְמַלְתָּא דִבְקִירְוֵיכוֹן וְיֵיכְלוּן וְיִסְבְּעוּן מִן בִּגְלַל דִּיבָרְכֵיכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּכָל עוֹבָדֵי יְדֵיכוֹן דְּתַעַבְדוּן


And the Levite, because he hath no part or heritage with you, and the stranger, the orphan, and the widow who are in your cities, shall come and eat and be satisfied; that the Lord your God may bless you in all the works of your hands that you do.



## Chapter 15

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:1)


מִסּוֹף שִׁבְעָתֵי שְׁנִין תַּעַבְדוּן שְׁמִיטָתָא


At the end of seven years you shall make a Release.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:2)


וְדֵין אַהֲוָויַת הִלְכַת שְׁמִיטָתָא אַשְׁמִיטוּ כָּל בַּר נַשׁ מָרֵי מְזוֹפְתָּא דְיִזוֹף בְּחַבְרֵיהּ לֵית לֵיהּ רְשׁוּ לִמְדַחֲקָא בְּחַבְרֵיהּ לְמִתְבּוֹעַ אוֹזְפוּתֵיהּ וְלָא מִן אָחוֹי בַּר יִשְרָאֵל אֲרוּם קָרָא בֵית דִּינָא שְׁמִיטָתָא קֳדָם יְיָ


And this is the indication of the custom of the Release: Every man who is master of a loan, who lendeth to his neighbour, shall give remission. He shall not have power to coerce his neighbour in demanding his loan, nor of his brother, a son of Israel; because the beth din hath published the Release before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:3)


יַת בַּר עַמְמִין תִּדְחוֹק וְדִינָא דִי יֶהֱוֵי לָךְ עִם אָחוּךְ תַּשְׁמֵיט יְדָךְ


From a son of the Gentiles thou mayest exact, but the lawful right (dina) which is thine with thy brother thou shalt release with thine hand.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:4)


לְחוֹד אִין אַתּוּן עֲסִיקִין בְּמִצְוָתָא דְאוֹרַיְיתָא לָא יֶהֱוֵי בְכוֹן מַסְכְּנָא אֲרוּם בְּרָכָא יְבָרְכֵכוּן יְיָ בְּאַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן אַחְסָנָא לְמֵירְתָהּ


If you will only be diligent in the precepts of the law, there will be no poor among you; for, blessing, the Lord will bless you in the land which the Lord your God will give you for a possession to inherit;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:5)


לְחוֹד אִין קַבָּלָא תְקַבְּלוּן לְמֵימְרָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְמִנְטוֹר וּלְמֶעֱבַד יַת כָּל תַּפְקֵידְתָּא הָדָא דַאֲנָא מְפַקֵּיד לְכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין


if, obeying, you will only obey the Word of the Lord your God, to observe and do all these commandments which I command you this day.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:6)


אֲרוּם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בֵּירַכְכוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְמַלֵּיל לְכוֹן וּתְמַשְׁכְּנוּן עַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין וְאַתּוּן לָא תִתְמַשְׁכְּנוּן וְתִשְׁלְטוּן בְּעַמְמִין סַגִּיאִין וּבְכוֹן לָא יִשְׁלְטוּן


For the Lord your God blesseth you, as He saith to you (that) you shall take from many nations, but they will not take from you; and you will have power over many nations, but they shall not have power over you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:7)


וְאִין לֵיתֵיכוֹן עֲסִיקִין בְּמִצְוָותָא דְאוֹרַיְיתָא וְיֶהֱוֵי בְּכוֹן מַסְכְּנָא מֵחַד אָחוּךְ בַּחֲדָא מִן קִרְוָוךְ בְּאַרְעָךְ דַיְיָ אֱלָהָךְ יָהֵיב לָךְ לָא תִתְקַף יַת לְבָבָךְ וְלָא תִמְנַע יַת אִידָךְ מֵאָחוּךְ מַסְכְּנָא


But if you be not diligent in the precepts of the law, and there be among you a poor man in one of thy cities of the land which the Lord thy God giveth thee, thou shalt not harden thy heart, nor hold back thy hand from thy poor brother;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:8)


אֶלָא מִפְתַּח תִּפְתַּח יַת אִידָךְ לֵיהּ וּמוּזְפָא תוּזְפִינֵיהּ הֵי כְּמֵיסַת חוּסְרָנֵיהּ דְּיֶיחְסַר לֵיהּ


but thou shalt open thy hand to him, and lend to him according to the measure of his want through which he is in need.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:9)


אִסְתַּמָּרוּ לְכוֹן דִּלְמָא יְהֵי פִּתְגָם עִם לִבְּכוֹן דְּזָדָנוּתָא לְמֵימָר קְרֵיבַת שַׁתָּא שְׁבִיעֲתָא שַׁתָּא דִשְׁמִיטְתָא וְתִבְאַשׁ עֵינֵיכוֹן בְּאָחוּכוֹן מִסְכֵּנָא וְלָא תִתְּנוּן לֵיהּ וְיִקְבּוֹל עֲלֵיכוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ וִיהֵי בְּכוֹן חוֹבָא


Beware lest there be a word in thy proud heart, saying: The seventh year, the year of release, is at hand, and your eye become evil toward your poor brother, so as to be not willing to give to him, and he cry against you to the Lord, and there be guilt upon you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:10)


מִתַּן תִּתְּנוּן לֵיהּ וְלָא יִבְאַשׁ לִבְּכוֹן בְּתִתְּנְכוֹן לֵיהּ אֲרוּם מְטוּל פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין יְבָרְכֵיכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּכָל עוֹבָדֵיכוֹן וּבְכָל אוֹשָׁטוּת יְדֵיכוֹן


Giving you shall give to him, nor shall your heart be evil when you give to him; for on account of this matter the Lord your God will bless you in all your works that you put your hands unto.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:11)


אֲרוּם מְטוּל דְּלָא צַיְיתִין בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל בְּמִצְוָותָא דְאוֹרַיְיתָא לָא פַסְקִין מַסְכְּנִין מִגּוֹ אַרְעָא בְּגִין כֵּן אֲנָא מְפַקִידְכוֹן לְמֵימָר מִפְתַּח תִּפְתְּחוּן יַת יְדֵיכוֹן לְקָרִיבֵיכוֹן וּלְעַנְיֵי שִׁיבְבוּתְכוֹן וּלְמִסְכְּנֵי אַרְעֲכוֹן


But forasmuch as the house of Israel will not rest in the commandments of the law, the poor will not cease in the land: therefore I command you, saying: You shall verily open your hands toward your neighbours, to the afflicted around you, and to the poor of your country.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:12)


אֲרוּם יִזְדַּבֵּן לְכוֹן אֲחוּכוֹן בַּר יִשְרָאֵל אוֹ בַת יִשְרָאֵל וְיִפְלִיחַנְכוֹן שִׁית שְׁנִין וּבְמַעֲלֵי שְׁבִיעֲתָא תִפְטְרוּנֵיהּ לְבַר חוֹרִין מִגַּבֵּיכוֹן


If your brother, a son of Israel, or if a daughter of Israel, be sold to you, he shall serve you six years; and when the seventh comes, thou shalt send him from you free.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:13)


וַאֲרוּם אִין תִּפְטְרוּנֵיהּ בַּר חוֹרֵי מִגַּבֵּיכוֹן לָא תִפְטְרוּנֵיהּ רֵיקָנָא


And when thou lettest him go away from thee at liberty, thou shalt not send him away empty.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:14)


מְדַחֲרָא תְדַחֲרוּן לֵיהּ מִן עַנְכוֹן מִן אִידְרֵיכוֹן וּמִן מַעֲצַרְתְּכוֹן דְּבָרִיכֵיכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן תִּתְּנוּן לֵיהּ


Comforting thou shalt comfort him out of your flocks, your floors, and your wine presses; as the Lord hath blessed you ye shall give to him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:15)


וְתֶהֱווֹן דְּכִירִין אֲרוּם מְשַׁעֲבְּדִין הֲוֵיתוּן בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וּפְרַקְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מְטוּל כֵּן אֲנָא מְפַקְדְּכוֹן לְמֶעֱבַד יַת פִּתְגָמָא הָדֵין יוֹמָא דֵין


And be mindful that you were servants in the land of Mizraim, and that the Lord your God set you free; therefore I command you today that you do this thing.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:16)


וִיהֵי אֲרוּם יֵימַר לָךְ לָא אֶפּוֹק מִן גַּבָּךְ אֲרוּם רַחֲמָךְ וְיַת אֵינַשׁ בֵּיתָךְ אֲרוּם טַב לֵיהּ דְּמִתְהָנֵי עִמָךְ


But if he say to thee, I will not go out from thee, because I love thee and the men of thy house, and because it hath been good for him to be with thee,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:17)


וְתִסַּב יַת מַחְטָא וְתִנְעוֹץ בְּאוּדְנֵיהּ וּבִתְרַע בֵּית דִינָא וִיהֵי לָךְ עֶבֶד פְּלַח עַד יוֹבְלָא וְאוּף לְאַמְתָךְ תִּכְתּוֹב גֵט חֵירַר וְתִתֵּן לָהּ


then thou shalt take an awl, and bore (or apply) it through his ear, and that to the door of the house of judgment, and he shall be thy serving servant until the Jubilee. And for thy handmaid also thou shalt write a certificate of release, and give it to her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:18)


לָא יִקְשֵׁי בְעֵינָךְ בְּמִפְטְרָךְ יָתֵיהּ לְבַר חוֹרִין מִן גַבָּךְ אֲרוּם בְּכוּפְלָא עַל אֲגַר אֲגִירָא פַּלְחָךְ שִׁית שְׁנִין וּבֵירְכָךְ מְטוּלֵיהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ בְּכָל מַה דְּתֶעֱבַד


It must not be a hardship in thy eyes when thou sendest him away from thee; for double the hire of an hireling hath he been of service to thee six years; and on his account the Lord thy God hath blessed thee in all that thou hast done.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:19)


כָּל בּוּכְרָא דְאִתְיְלִיד בְּתוֹרָךְ וּבְעָנָךְ דּוּכְרַיָא תַּקְדֵּישׁ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָךְ לָא תִפְלוֹחַ בְּבִכּוּרֵי תּוֹרֵיכוֹן וְלָא תֵיגוֹז בִּכּוּרֵי עָנֵיכוֹן


Every firstling male that cometh of thy herd and flock thou shalt consecrate before the Lord thy God. Thou shalt not work with the firstlings of your herd, nor shear the firstlings of your flocks;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:20)


קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן תֵּיכְלִינֵיהּ שְׁנָא בִּשְׁנָא בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אַתּוּן וְאֵינַשׁ בָּתֵּיכוֹן


you shall eat thereof before the Lord your God from year to year, in the place which the Lord will choose, you and the men of your houses.

וַאֲרוּם יְהֵי בֵּיהּ מוּמָא חֲגִיר אוֹ סָמֵי כָּל מִדַּעַם בִּישׁ לָא תִכְסִינֵיהּ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


But if there be any spot in it, if it be lame or blind, or have any blemish, you shall not sacrifice it before the Lord your God:

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/15:22)


בְּקִרְוֵיכוֹן תֵּיכְלִינֵיהּ דִּמְסָאָב מִן לְמִקְרַב לְקוּדְשַׁיָא וְדִדְכֵי לְמִקְרַב לְקוּדְשַׁיָא כַּחֲדָא הֵי כִּבְשַר טַבְיָא וְאַיְלָא


you may eat it in your cities; he who is unclean, (so) that he may not approach to holy things, and he who being clean may approach the holy, may alike (eat), as the flesh of the antelope or hart.

לְחוֹד אַדְמֵיהּ לָא תֵיכְלוּן עַל אַרְעָא תֵישְׁדוּנֵיהּ הֵי כְמַיָא
