## Chapter 16

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:1)


הֲווֹן זְהִירִין לְמִנְטוֹר זִמְנֵי מוֹעֲדַיָא לְעִבּוּרֵי שַׁתָּא וּלְמִנְטוֹר תְּקוּפְתָּא בְּיַרְחָא דְאַבִּיבָא לְמֶעֱבַד בֵּיהּ פִּסְחָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן אֲרוּם בְּיַרְחָא דְאַבִּיבָא אַפֵּיק יַתְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מִמִּצְרַיִם וְתֵיכְלוּן יָתֵיהּ בְּלֵילְיָא


Be mindful to keep the times of the festivals, with the intercalations of the year, and to observe the rotation thereof: in the month of Abiba to perform the pascha before the Lord your God, because in the month of Abiba the Lord your God brought you out of Mizraim; you shall eat it therefore by night.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:2)


וְתִיכְסוּן פִּסְחָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בֵּינֵי שִׁמְשָׁתָא וְעָאן וְתוֹרֵי לִמְחָר בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא לְחֶדְוַת חַגָּא בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן


But you shall sacrifice the pascha before the Lord your God between the suns; and the sheep and the bullocks on the morrow, on that same day to rejoice in the feast at the place which the Lord will choose to make His Shekinah to dwell there.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:3)


לָא תֵיכְלוּן עַל פִּסְחָא חֲמִיעַ שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין תֵּיכְלוּן לִשְׁמֵיהּ פַּטִּיר לַחֲמָא עַנְיָא אֲרוּם בִּבְהִילוּ נְפַקְתּוּן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מִן בִּגְלַל דְּתִידְכְּרוּן יַת יוֹם מַפְקְכוֹן מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם כָּל יוֹמֵי חַיֵּיכוֹן


You shall not eat leavened bread with the pascha; seven days you shall eat unleavened bread unto His Name, the unleavened bread of humiliation; for with haste you went forth from the land of Mizraim; that you may remember the day of your outgoing from the land of Mizraim all the days of your life.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:4)


וְתִזְדַהֲרוּן מִקַּמֵּי פִּסְחָא דְלָא יִתְחָמֵי לְכוֹן חֲמִיר בְּכָל תְּחוּמְכוֹן שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין וְלָא יְבִית בַּר מִן בִּשְרָא דְתִכְסוּן בְּרַמְשָׁא בְּיוֹמָא קַמָּאָה לְצַפְרָא


Take heed that in the beginning of the pascha there be no leaven seen among you within all your borders for seven days; and that none of the flesh which you sacrifice in the evening of the first day remain till the morning.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:5)


לֵית לְכוֹן רְשׁוּ לְמִכּוֹס יַת פִּסְחָא בַּחֲדָא מִן קִירְוֵיכוֹן דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן


It will not be allowed you to eat the pascha in (any) one of your cities which the Lord your God giveth to you;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:6)


אֱלָהֵן בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן תִּיכְסוּן יַת פִּסְחָא וּבְרַמְשָׁא בְּמֵיעֲלִית שִׁמְשָׁא תֵּיכְלוּנֵיהּ עַד פַּלְגוּת לֵילְיָא זְמַן שַׁרְיוּת פּוּרְקַנְכוֹן מִמִּצְרָיִם


but in the place which the Lord your God will choose to make His Shekinah to dwell, there shall you sacrifice the pascha; and in the evening at the going down of the sun you may eat it until the middle of the night, the time when you began to go out of Mizraim.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:7)


וְתִטְווּן וְתֵיכְלוּן בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בֵּיהּ וְתִתְפְּנֵי בְּצַפְרָא מֵיפַק חַגָא וּתְהַךְ לְקִרְוָוךְ


And you shall dress and eat it in the place which the Lord your God will choose, and in the early morn (if need be) thou mayest return from the feast, and go to thy cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:8)


בְּיוֹמָא קַמָּא תְקַרְבוּן יַת עוֹמְרָא וְתֵיכְלוּן פַּטִּירֵי מַעֲלַלְתָּא עַתִּיקָא וְשִׁתָּא יוֹמִין דְּאִשְׁתְּיָירוּ תְּהוֹן מַרְשַׁן לְמֵיכוֹל פַּטוּרֵי מַעֲלַלְתָּא חֲדַתָּא וּבְיוֹמָא שְׁבִיעָאָה הַהוּא תְּהוֹן כְּנִישִׁין בְּתוּשְׁבְּחָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לָא תַעַבְדוּן עִיבִידְתָּא


On the first day thou shalt offer the omer, and eat unleavened cakes of the old corn; but in the six remaining days you may begin to eat unleavened cakes of the new corn, and on the seventh day you shall assemble with thanksgiving before the Lord your God; no work shall you perform.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:9)


שׁוּבְעָא שְׁבוּעִין תִּימְנוּן לְכוֹן מִזְמַן דְּתִשְׁרוּן לְמִשְׁלַח מַגְלָא לְמַחֲצוֹד בְּחַקְלָא בָּתַר חֲצַד עוֹמְרָא תֵּישְׁרוּן לְמִמְנֵי שׁוּבְעָא שְׁבוּעִין


Seven weeks number to you; from the time when you begin to put the sickle to the harvest of the field after the reaping of the omer you shall begin to number the seven weeks.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:10)


וְתַעַבְדוּן חֶדְוַת חַגָּא דְשָׁבוּעַיָיא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן הֵי כְּמֵסַת נִדְבַת יְדֵיכוֹן הֵיכְמָא דְבָרְכֵיכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


And you shall keep with joy the Festival of Weeks before the Lord your God, after the measure of the freewill offerings of your hands, according as the Lord your God shall have blessed you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:11)


וְתֵיחְדוּן בְּחֶדְוַת חַגָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן אַתּוּן וּבְנֵיכוֹן וּבְנָתֵיכוֹן וְעַבְדֵיכוֹן וְאַמְהָתֵיכוֹן וְלֵוָאֵי דִי בְקִרְוֵיכוֹן וְגִיּוֹרָא וְיַתְמָא וְאַרְמַלְתָּא דִי בֵינֵיכוֹן בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְאַשְׁרָאָה שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ תַּמָּן


And you shall rejoice with the joy of the feast before the Lord your God, you and your sons, your daughters, your servants and handmaids, the Levites who are in your cities, and the stranger, the orphan, and the widow who are among you, at the place which the Lord your God will choose where to make His Shekinah to dwell.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:12)


וְתֶהֱווֹן דְּכִירִין אֲרוּם מְשַׁעְבְּדִין הֲוֵיתוּן בְּמִצְרַיִם וְתִיטְרוּן וְתַעַבְדוּן יַת קְיָימַיָא הָאִלֵין


Remember that you were servants in Mizraim; so shall you observe and perform these statutes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:13)


חַגָא דִמְטָלַיָא תַעַבְּדוּן לְכוֹן שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמִין בְּמִשְׁלַמְכוֹן לְמִכְנוֹשׁ עֲלַלְתָּא מֵאִידִירֵכוֹן וְחַמְרָא מִן מַעֲצַרְתֵּיכוֹן


The Feast of Tabernacles you shall make to you seven days, when you will have completed to gather in the corn from your threshing floors, and the wine from your presses.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:14)


וְתֵיחְדוּן בְּחֶדְוַת חַגֵּיכוֹן בְּשָׁאוּבְתָּא וְחָלִילָא אַתּוּן וּבְנֵיכוֹן וּבְנָתֵיכוֹן וְאַמְהוֹתֵיכוֹן וְלֵיוָאָה וְגִיּוֹרָא וְיַתְמָא וְאַרְמַלְתָּא דִי בְקִרְוֵיכוֹן


And you shall rejoice in the joy of your feasts with the clarinet and flute, you and your sons and daughters, your handmaids, the Levite, the stranger, the orphan, and the widow, who are in your cities.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:15)


שׁוּבְעָא יוֹמָא תְּחַגּוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֲרוּם יְבָרֵיכְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּכָל עֲלַלְתְּכוֹן וּבְכָל עוֹבָדֵי אִידֵיכוֹן וְתֶהֱווֹן בְּרַם חַדְוָן בְּאַצְלָחוּתָא


Seven days you shall keep the feast before the Lord your God in the place which the Lord will choose, because the Lord your God will have blessed you in all your provision, and in all the work of your hands, and so shall you be joyful in prosperity.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:16)


תְּלַת זִימְנִין בְּשַׁתָּא יִתְחֲמוּן כָּל דְּכוּרֵיכוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי בְּחַגָּא דְפַטִּירַיָא וּבְחַגָּא דְשָׁבוּעַיָא וּבְחַגָּא דִמְטָלַיָא וְלֵיתֵיכוֹן רַשָּׁאִין לְמִתְחַמְיָא קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן רֵיקָנוּן מִכָּל מִצְוָתָא


Three times in the year shall all your males appear before the Lord your God in the place that He will choose; at the Feast of the Unleavened, at the Feast of Weeks, and at the Feast of Tabernacles; nor must you appear before the Lord your God empty of any of the requirements;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:17)


גְּבַר הֵי כְּמֵיסַת מוֹהֲבוֹת יְדֵיהּ כְּבִרְכָתָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דִּיהַב לְכוֹן


every one after the measure of the gifts of his hands, according to the blessing which the Lord your God hath bestowed upon you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:18)


שופטים דַּיָּנִין קְשׁוּטִין וְסַרְכִין אַלִּימִין תְּמַנּוּן לְכוֹן בְּכָל קִירְוֵיכוֹן דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהִיב לְכוֹן לְשִׁבְטֵיכוֹן וִידוּנוּן יַת עַמָּא דִּין קְשׁוֹט


UPRIGHT judges and efficient administrators you shall appoint in all your cities which the Lord your God will give you for your tribes, and they shall judge the people with true judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:19)


לָא תֵיצְלוּן דִּינָא וְלָא תִסְבּוּן אַפִּין וְלָא תְקַבְּלוּן שׁוֹחֲדָא אֲרוּם שׁוֹחֲדָא מְסַמֵּי עֵינֵי חַכִּימַיָא דְנַסְבִין לֵיהּ דְּגָרִים לְהוֹן טִפְּשׁוּתָא וּמְעַרְבֵּב מִילִין זַכָּאִין בְּפוּם דַּיָּינַיָא בְּשַׁעַת דִּינֵיהוֹן


You shall not set judgment aside, nor respect persons, nor take a gift, because a gift blindeth the eyes of the wise who take it; for it perverteth them to foolishness, and confuseth equitable words in the mouth of the judges in the hour of their decision


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:20)


דִּין קְשׁוֹט וְדִין שְׁלַם בִּקְשׁוֹט תְּהֵי רָדִיף מִן בִּגְלַל דְּתֵיחוּן וְתֵירְתוּן יַת אַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן


Upright and perfect judgment in truth shalt thou follow, that you may come to inherit the land which the Lord your God will give you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:21)


הֵיכְמָא דְלֵיתֵיכוֹן רַשָּׁאִין לְמִנְצוֹב אֲשֵׁירָתָא לִסְטַר מַדְבְּחָא דַיְיָ הֵיכְדֵין לֵיתֵיכוֹן רַשָּׁאִין לִמְזַוְוגָא בְּדִינָא גַבְרָא טִפְּשָׁא עִם דַּיָינַיָא חַכִּימַיָא לְמַלִיפָא לְכוֹן יַת דְּתַעַבְּדוּן לְכוֹן


As it is not allowed you to plant a grove by the side of the Lord’s altar, so is it not allowed you to associate in judgment a fool with a wise judge to teach that which you are to do.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 16:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/16:22)


וְהֵיכְמָא דְלֵיתֵיכוֹן רְשָׁאִין לְמִקְמָא קָמָא הֵיכְדֵין לֵיתֵיכוֹן רַשָּׁאִין לְמַנָאָה לְפַרְנָסָא גַּבְרָא זִידְנָא דְרַחֲקֵיהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


As it is not for you to erect a statue, so are you not to appoint to be a governor a proud man, whom the Lord your God doth abhor.



## Chapter 17

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:1)


לָא תִכְסוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן תּוֹר וְאִימַר דִּיהֵי בֵיהּ מוּמָא אוֹ כָּל מִדַּעַם בִּישׁ דְּגָזִיל וְאָנִיס אֲרוּם מְרַחֵק קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן הוּא


You shall not sacrifice before the. Lord your God a bullock or lamb which hath any blemish or evil in it, or which is torn or rent; for that is abominable before the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:2)


אֲרוּם יִשְׁתַּכַּח בֵּינֵיכוֹן בַּחֲדָא מִן קוּרְיֵיכוֹן דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן גְּבַר אוֹ אִיתָא דְּעָבַד דְּבִישׁ קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְמֵעִיבַר עַל קְיָימֵיהּ


If there be found among you in one of your cities that the Lord your God will give you a man or woman who doth what is evil before the Lord your God in transgressing His covenant,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:3)


וַאֲזַל בָּתַר יִצְרָא בִּישָׁא וּפְלַח לְטַעֲוַות עַמְמַיָא וּסְגִיד לְהוֹן וּלְשִׁמְשָׁא אוֹ לְסִיהֲרָא אוֹ לְכָל חֵילָא שְׁמַיָא דְלָא פַקֵּידִית


and, following after evil desire, shall serve the idols of the Gentiles, and worship them, or the sun, or the moon, or all the host of the heavens, which I have not commanded;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:4)


וְאִתְחַוָא לְכוֹן וְתִשְׁמְעוּן וְתִיתְבְּעוּן יַת סַהֲדַיָא טַבְאוּת וְהָא קוּשְׁטָא כֵּיוַן פִּתְגָמָא אִתְעֲבִידַת תּוֹעֵבְתָּא הָדָא בֵּינֵיכוֹן


and it be told you, and you hear and make inquiry by witnesses fairly; and, behold, if this word be true and certain, that such abomination is wrought among you,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:5)


וְתִפְקוּן יַת גַּבְרָא הַהוּא אוֹ יַת אִנְתְּתָא הַהִיא דְּעָבְדוּ יַת פִּתְגָמָא בִּישָׁא הָדֵין לִתְרַע בֵּית דִּינֵיכוֹן יַת גַּבְרָא אוֹ יַת אִינְתְּתָא וּתְאַטְלוּנוּן בְּאַבְנַיָא וִימוּתוּן


then you shall bring forth that man or woman who hath done this evil thing, unto the gate of your house of judgment, the man or the woman, and you shall stone them that they die.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:6)


עַל מֵימַר תְּרֵין סַהֲדִין אוֹ תְּלָתָא סַהֲדִין יִתְקְטֵיל דְּמִתְחַיֵּיב קָטוֹלָא לָא יִתְקְטֵיל עַל מֵימַר סָהִיד חָד


Upon the word of two witnesses or of three he shall die who is guilty of death; they shall not be put to death on the word of one witness.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:7)


יְדָא דְסַהֲדַיָא תְהֵי בֵיהּ בְּשֵׁירוּיָא לְמִקְטְלֵיהּ וִידָא דְכָל עַמָּא בְּבַתְרֵיתָא וּתְפַלּוּן עֲבִיד דְּבִישׁ מִבֵּינֵיכוֹן


The hands of the witnesses shall be first upon him to kill him, and afterward the hands of all (any of) the people; and so shall you bring down the evil doer among you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:8)


אֲרוּם יִתְפְּרֵשׁ מִנְכוֹן פִּתְגָמָא לְדִינָא בֵּינֵי אֲדַם סְאוֹב לַאֲדַם דְּכֵי וּבֵינֵי דִינָא נַפְשָׁתָא לְדִינָא מָמוֹנָא וּבֵינֵי מַכְתַּשׁ צוֹרְעָא לְמַכְתַּשׁ נִתְקָא מִלֵי פְּלוּגְתָּא בְּבֵית דִּינֵיכוֹן וּתְקוּמוּן וְתִסְקוּן לְאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בֵּיהּ


If there be with you an extraordinary matter for judgment between unclean and clean blood, cases of life or of money, or between a plague of leprosy or of the scall, with words of controversy in your beth din, then you shall arise and go up to the place which the Lord your God will choose;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:9)


וְתֵיתוּן לְוַת כַּהֲנַיָא דְמִשֵּׁבֶט לֵוִי וּלְוַת דַּיָּינָא דִי יְהֵי בְיוֹמַיָא הָאִינוּן וְתִתְבְּעוּן מִנְהוֹן וִיחַוּוּן לְכוֹן יַת הִלְכַת דִּינָא


and you shall come to the priests of the tribe of Levi, and to the judge who will be in those days, and inquire of them, and they will show you the process of judgment.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:10)


וְתַעַבְדוּן עַל מֵימַר הִלְכַת אוֹרַיְיתָא דִיחַוּוּן לְכוֹן מִן אַתְרָא הַהוּא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ וְתִטְרוּן לְמֶעֱבַד כְּכָל דְּיַלְפוּנְכוֹן


Then shall you do according to the word of the custom of the law that they will show you at the place the Lord will choose, and observe to do whatsoever they teach you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:11)


עַל מֵימַר אוֹרַיְיתָא דְיַלְפוּנְכוֹן וְעַל הִילְכַת דִּינָא דְיֵימְרוּן לְכוֹן תַּעַבְדוּן לָא תִסְטוּן מִן פִּתְגָמָא דִיחַוּוּן לְכוֹן יְמִינָא וּשְמָאלָא


According to the word of the law that they will teach you, and the manner of judgment they pronounce, you shall do. You shall not turn aside from the sentence they will show you, to the right or to the left.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:12)


וְגַבְרָא דְיַעֲבֵיד בְּזָדְנוּתָא בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְמֵיצַת מִן כַּהֲנָא דְקָאִים לְשַׁמָּשָׁא תַּמָּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן אוֹ מִן דַּיָינָא וְיִתְקְטֵיל גַּבְרָא הַהוּא וּתְפַלּוּן עֲבִיד דְּבִישׁ מִיִּשְרָאֵל


And the man who will act with presumption, and not obey the judge or the priest who standeth there to minister before the Lord your God, that man shall be put to death; so shalt you put down the doer of evil from Israel,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:13)


וְכָל עַמָּא יִשְׁמְעוּן וִידַחֲלוּן וְלָא יַרְשִׁיעוּן תּוּב


and all the people will hear, and be afraid, and not do wickedly again.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:14)


אֲרוּם תֵּיעָלוּן לְאַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן וְתֵרְתוּן יָתָהּ וְתֵיתְבוּן בָּהּ וְתֵמְרוּן נְמַנֵי עֲלָן מַלְכָּא כְּכָל עַמְמַיָא דִבְחַזְרָנוּתָי


When you enter the land which the Lord your God giveth you, and possess, and dwell in it, and you say, Let us appoint a king over us, like all the nations about me,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:15)


תִּתְבְּעוּן אוּלְפַן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ וּמִבָּתַר כְּדֵין תִּמְנוּן עֲלֵיכוֹן מַלְכָּא לֵית לְכוֹן רְשׁוּ לְמַנָּאָה עֲלֵיכוֹן גְּבַר חִילוֹנַי דְלָא מִן אֲחוּכוֹן הוּא


you shall inquire for instruction before the Lord and afterward appoint the king over you: but it will not be lawful to set over you a foreign man who is not of your brethren.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:16)


לְחוֹד לָא יִסְגוּן לֵיהּ עַל תְּרֵין סוּסְוָון דִּלְמָא יִרְכְּבוּן רַבְרְבָנוֹי עֲלֵיהוֹן וְיִתְגָאוּן וְיִתְבַּטְלוּן מִפִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא וִיחוֹבוּן חוֹבַת גָּלוּתָא לְמִצְרַיִם וַיְיָ אָמַר לְכוֹן לָא תוֹסְפוּן לְמֵתוּב בְּאָרְחָא הָדֵין תּוּב


Only let him not increase to him more than two horses, lest his princes ride upon them, and become proud, neglect the words of the law, and commit the sin of the captivity of Mizraim; for the Lord hath told you, By that way ye shall return no more.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:17)


וְלָא יִסְגוּן לֵיהּ נְשִׁין עַל תַּמְנַסְרֵי דְּלָא יִטְעוּן לִבֵּיהּ וְכַסְפָּא וְדַהֲבָא לָא יִסְגֵי לֵיהּ דְּלָא יִתְרוֹמָם לִבֵּיהּ לַחֲדָא וְיִמְרוֹד בֶּאֱלָהָא שְׁמַיָא


Neither shall he multiply to him wives above eighteen, lest they pervert his heart; nor shall he increase to him silver or gold, lest his heart be greatly lifted up, and he rebel against the God of heaven.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:18)


וִיהֵי אִין נְיַיח הוּא בְּמִצְוָותָא דְאוֹרַיְיתָא יָתֵיב לְרוֹחֲצָן עַל כּוּרְסֵי מַלְכוּתֵיהּ וְיִכְתְּבוּן לֵיהּ סָבַיָא יַת פַּרְשָׁא אוֹרַיְיתָא הָדָא עַל סִפְרָא מִן קֳדָם כַּהֲנַיָא דְּמִן שֵׁבֶט לֵוִי


And it shall be that if he be steadfast in the commandments of the law he shall sit upon the throne of his kingdom in security. And let the elders write for him the section (pharasha) of this law in a book before the priests of the tribe of Levi;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:19)


וּתְהֵי גַבֵּיהּ וִיהֵי קָרֵי בֵּיהּ כָּל יוֹמֵי חַייוֹי מִן בִּגְלַל דְּיַלִּיף לְמִדְחַל מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהֵיהּ לְמִנְטַל יַת כָּל פִּתְגָמֵי אוֹרַיְיתָא הָדָא וְיַת כָּל קְיָימַיָא הָאִלֵּין לְמֶעֱבַדְהוֹן


and let it be at his side, and he shall read it all the days of his life, that he may learn to fear the Lord his God, to keep all the words of this law, and all these statutes to perform them:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/17:20)


מְטוּל דְּלָא יָגִיס לִבֵּיהּ יַתִּיר מִן אָחוֹי וּמְטוּל דְּלָא יִסְטֵי מִן תַּפְקֵידְתָּא לְיַמִינָא וְלִשְמָאלָא מִן בִּגְלַל דְּיִנְגוֹד יוֹמִין עַל מַלְכוּתֵיהּ הוּא וּבְנוֹי בֵּינֵי יִשְרָאֵל


that his heart may not be arrogant toward his brethren, nor decline from the precepts to the right or the left, and that his days may be prolonged over his kingdom, his and his sons among Israel.



## Chapter 18

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:1)


לָא יְהֵי לְכַהֲנַיָא דְּמִן שֵׁבֶט לֵוִי חוּלַק וְאַחְסָנָא עִם אָחוֹי קוּרְבָּנַיָא דַיְיָ וּבְאַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן יֵיכְלוּן


The priests of the tribe of Levi will have no part or inheritance with their brethren: they shall eat the oblations of the Lord as their portion,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:2)


וְאַחֲסָנַת חֲקַל וּכְרַם לָא תְהֵי לֵיהּ בֵּינֵי אָחוֹי עֶשְרִין וְאַרְבַּע מוֹהֲבוּתָא דִכְהוּנְתָּא דִיהַב לֵיהּ יְיָ הִינוּן אַחְסַנְתֵּיהּ הֵיכְמָא דְמַלִּיל לֵיהּ


but an inheritance in field or vineyard they will not have among their brethren. The twenty and four gifts of the priesthood which the Lord will give to him are his heritage; as He said to him,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:3)


וְדֵין יְהֵי חוּלְקָא דְּחָמֵי לְכַהֲנַיָא מִן עַמָּא מִלְוַת דִּבְחֵי דִבְחִין אִין תּוֹר אִין אִימַר וְיִתְּנוּן לְכַהֲנָא אִדְרָעָא דְיַמִּינָא וְלוּחַיָא אַרְעֲיָא וְלוּחַיָא דִרְקִיתָא וְקֵיבְתָא


And this shall be the portion belonging to the priest from the people, from them who offer sacrifices, whether bullock or lamb they shall give to the priest the right shoulder, the lower jaw, the cheeks, and the maw;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:4)


שֵׁירוּי עִיבּוּרֵיכוֹן חַמְרֵיכוֹן וּמִשְׁחֵיכוֹן וְשֵׁירוּי גִּיזַת עָנֵיכוֹן כְּמִסַת קָטוֹרָא תִּתְּנוּן לֵיהּ


the firsts of your corn, wine, and oil, the first of the fleece of your sheep, as much as a girdle measureth shall you give to him:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:5)


מְטוּל דְּבֵיהּ אִתְרְעֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מִכָּל שִׁבְטֵיכוֹן לְמֵיקוּם לְשַׁמָּשָׁא בִּשְׁמָא דַיְיָ הוּא וּבְנוֹי כָּל יוֹמַיָא


because the Lord thy God hath chosen him out of all thy tribes to stand and minister in the Name of the Lord, him, and his sons, all the days.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:6)


וַאֲרוּם יֵיתֵי לֵיוָאָה מֵחֲדָא מִן קִרְוֵיכוֹן מִן כָּל יִשְרָאֵל דְּהוּא דַיָיר תַּמָּן וְיֵיתֵי בְּכָל כְּרוּךְ דְּרָעֲיָא נַפְשֵׁיהּ לְאַתְרָא דְיִתְרְעֵי יְיָ


And when a Levite may come from one of your cities out of all Israel where he hath dwelt, and come with all the obligation of his soul’s desire to the place which the Lord will choose,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:7)


וִישַׁמֵּשׁ בְּשׁוּם מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהֵיהּ הֵי כְּכָל אָחוֹי לֵיוָאֵי דִמְשַׁמְשִׁין תַּמָּן קֳדָם יְיָ


then he shall minister in the Name of the Lord his God as all his brethren the Levites who minister there before the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:8)


חוּלַק כָּל קֳבֵיל חוּלַק בְּשָׁוֶה יֵיכְלוּן בַּר מִמּוֹתְבֵי קוּרְבָּנַיָא דְיֵיכְלוּן כַּהֲנַיָא דְאוֹרִיתוּ לְהוֹן אֶלְעָזָר וְאִיתָמָר אֲבָהַתְכוֹן


Portion for portion equally shall they eat, besides the gifts of the oblations which the priests do eat, which Elazar and Ithamar your fathers have given them to inherit.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:9)


אֲרוּם אַתּוּן עַלְלִין לְאַרְעָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן לָא תֵילְפוּן לְמֶעֱבַד כְּרִיחוֹקֵי עַמְמַיָא הָאִינוּן


When you have entered the land which the Lord your God giveth you, ye shall not learn to do after the abominations of those nations.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:10)


לָא יִשְׁתְּכַח בְּכוֹן מְעַבְּרִין בְּנֵיהוֹן וּבְנָתֵיהוֹן בְּנוּרָא לָא קְסוֹמֵי קוּסְמִין וְלָא חֲרוּרֵי עַיְינִין וְלָא נַטוֹרֵי נְחָשִׁין וְלָא חַרְשִׁין


None shall be found among you to make his sons or daughters pass through the fire, nor who enchant with enchantments, or inspect serpents, nor observe divinations and auguries,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:11)


וּמְחַבְּרִין וְאַסְרִין חֵיוִין וְעַקְרַבִּין וְכָל מִינֵי רַחֲשִׁין וְשַׁאֲלִין אִיבָא טַמְיָא וּגְרַם יָדוּעַ וּתְבַע מִן מֵיתַיָא


or make (magical) knots and bindings of serpents and scorpions or any kind of reptile, or who consult the oba, the bones of the dead or the bone Jadua, or who inquire of the manes.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:12)


אֲרוּם מְרַחֵק קֳדָם יְיָ כָּל עֲבֵיד אִלֵין וּמְטוֹל תּוֹעֵיבְתָּא הָאִילֵין יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מְתָרֵיכֵיהוֹן מִן קֳדָמֵיכוֹן


For every one who doeth these is an abomination before the Lord; and because of these abominations the Lord driveth them out before you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:13)


שַׁלְמִין תְּהוֹן בְּדַחַלְתָּא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


Ye shall be perfect in the fear of the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:14)


אֲרוּם עַמְמַיָא הָאִלֵין דְּאַתּוּן עֲתִידִין לְמֵירוֹת יַתְהוֹן לְחַרוֹרֵי עֵינָא וּלְקִיסוּמֵי קוּסְמַיָא צַיְיתוּן וְאַתּוּן לָא כְוַותְהוֹן אֱלָהֵן כַּהֲנַיָא שַׁיְילוּן אוּרַיָא וְתוּמַיָא וְנָבִיָא תְרִיצַיָא יְהַב לְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


For these nations which thou art about to dispossess have listened to inspectors of serpents and enchanters. But you are not to be like them the priests shall inquire by Urim and Thummim and a Right Prophet will the Lord your God give you;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:15)


נְבִיָא מִבֵּינֵיכוֹן מִן אֲחוּכוֹן דְּדָמֵי לִי בְּרוּחַ קוּדְשָׁא יְקִים לְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן מִנֵּיהּ תְקַבְּלוּן


a Prophet from among you of your brethren like unto me, with the Holy Spirit will the Lord your God raise up unto you; to Him shall you be obedient.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:16)


כְּכל דְּשַׁיִּילְתּוּן מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּחוֹרֵב בְּיוֹמָא דְאִתְכַּנָּשׁוּ שִׁבְטַיָא לִמְקַבְּלָא אוֹרַיְיתָא לְמֵימָר לָא נוֹסִיף לְמִשְׁמַע יַת קַל דִּיבּוּרָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָנָא וְיַת אֵישָׁתָא רַבְּתָא הֲדָא לָא נֶחֱמֵי תּוּב דְּלָא נְמוּת


According to all that you begged before the Lord your God in Horeb on the day of the assembling of the tribes to receive the law, saying, Let us not again hear the Great Voice {of the Word - Dibbura} from before the Lord our God, nor behold again that great fire, lest we die:

וַאֲמַר יְיָ לִי אַתְקִינָן מַה דְּמַלֵּילָן


and the Lord said to me, That which they have spoken is right;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:18)


נְבִיָא אָקִים לְהוֹן מִבֵּינֵי אֲחוּהוֹן דְּרוּחַ קוּדְשָׁא בֵּיהּ כְּוָותָךְ וְאֶתֵּן פִּתְגָמֵי נְבוּאָתִי בְּפוּמֵיהּ וִימַלֵּיל עִמְהוֹן יַת כָּל דְּאִיפְקְדִינֵיהּ


I will raise up unto them a Prophet from, among their brethren in whom shall be the Holy Spirit, as in thee; and I will put My Word of prophecy in his mouth, and he shall speak with them whatsoever I command him;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:19)


וִיהֵי גַבְרָא דְלָא יִתְקַבֵּל פִּתְגָמֵי נְבוּאָתִי דִימַלֵּיל בִּשְׁמִי מֵימְרִי יִפְרַע מִנֵּיהּ


and the man who will not hearken to the words of My prophecy which shall be spoken in My Name, My Word shall take vengeance upon him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:20)


בְּרַם נְבִי שִׁקְרָא דְיִרְשַׁע לְמַלָלָא פִּתְגָמָא בִּשְׁמִי יַת דְּלָא פַקִידְתֵּיהּ לְמַלָּלָא וְדִי יְמַלֵּל בְּשׁוּם טַעֲוַות עַמְמַיָא וְיִתְקְטֵיל נְבִיָא הַהוּא בְּסַיְיפָא


But the false prophet who doeth wickedly in speaking a thing in My Name, when I have not commanded him to speak, or who shall speak in the name of the gods of the Gentiles., that prophet shall be slain with the sword.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:21)


וַאֲרוּם תֵּימְרוּן בְּרַעֲיוֹנֵיכוֹן הֵיכְדֵין נֵידַע יַת פִּתְגָּמָא דְלָא מַלְלֵיהּ יְיָ


And if thou shalt say in your thoughts, How shall we know the word which the Lord hath not spoken?


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/18:22)


מַה דִּימַלֵּיל נְבִי שִׁקְרָא בִּשְׁמָא דַיְיָ וְלָא יֵיתֵי פִּתְגָּמָא וְלָא יִתְקַיֵּים הוּא פִּתְגָּמָא דְּלָא מַלְלֵיהּ יְיָ בִּזְדָנוּתָא מַלְלֵיהּ נְבִי שִׁקְרָא לָא תִדַחֲלוּן מִנֵּיהּ


When a false prophet speaketh in the Name of the Lord, and the thing doth not come to pass, or be not confirmed, it is a word which the Lord hath not spoken; the false prophet spake it in presumption; fear him not.



## Chapter 19

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:1)


אֲרוּם יְשֵׁיצֵי יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יַת עַמְמַיָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן יַת אַרְעֲהוֹן וְתֵירְתִינוּן וְתֵיתְבוּן בְּקִירְוֵיהוֹן וּבְבָתֵּיהוֹן


When the Lord your God shall have destroyed the nations whose land the Lord your God giveth you, and you possess them, and dwell in their cities and houses,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:2)


תְּלַת קִרְוִין תִּפְרְשׁוּן לְכוֹן בְּגוֹ אַרְעֲכוֹן דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן לְמֵירְתָהּ


three cities shall you set apart within your land which the Lord your God giveth you to inherit.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:3)


תְּכַוְונוּן לְכוֹן אָרְחָא וּתְתַלְתוּן יַת תְּחוּם אַרְעֲכוֹן דִּיחַסְנְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וִיהֵי לְמַעֲרוֹק לְתַמָּן כָּל קָטוֹלָא


You shall prepare a high road, and divide your limit which the Lord your God bestoweth upon you, that any manslayer may flee thither.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:4)


וְדֵין הִילְכַת קָטוֹלָא דְיַעֲרוֹק לְתַמָּן וְיֵיחֵי דְּיִקְטוֹל יַת אָחֵיהּ בְּלָא מִתְכַוִין לֵיהּ וְהוּא לָא נְטַר לֵיהּ סְנָא מֵאִתְמְלֵי וּמִדְקֳדָמוֹי


And this is the regulation for the manslayer who fleeth thither that he may live: Whoever shall have killed his brother without intention, he not having kept enmity against him yesterday, or the day before,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/19:5)


וּמַאן דְּעָלֵיל עִם חַבְרֵיהּ בְּחֻרְשָׁא לְמִקְטַע קִיסִין וְתִדְחַף אִידֵיהּ בְּסִיקוֹרְיָא לְמִקְטַע קִיסָא וְיִשְׁמוֹט פַּרְזְלָא מִן קַתָּא וְיַשְׁכַּח יַת חַבְרֵיהּ וִימוּת הוּא יַעֲרוֹק לַחֲדָא מִן קִירְוַיָא מְזַמְנַיָיא הָאִלֵּין וְיֵיחֵי


(as for example) if any one goeth with his neighbour into the thicket to cut wood, and he driveth his hand with the axe to cut wood, and the iron flieth apart from the haft and lighteth on his neighbour that be die, he may flee to one of those appointed cities, and. save his life.

דִּלְמָא יִרְדוֹף תְּבַע אַדְמָא בָּתַר קָטוֹלָא אֲרוּם יִרְתַח עֲלוֹי לִבֵּיהּ בְּעַקְתֵיהּ וְיִדְבְּקֵיהּ אֲרוּם תַּסְגֵי אָרְחָא וְיִקְטְלִינֵיהּ נְפָשׁ וְלֵיהּ לָא אִית חוֹבַת דִּין דִּקְטוֹל אֲרוּם לָא סָנֵי הוּא לֵיהּ מֵאִתְמְלֵי וּמִדְקֳדָמוֹי


Lest the avenger of blood follow after him his heart boiling within him on account of his grief, and apprehend him, if the way be long, and take his life, though he is not guilty of the judgment of death, because he had not enmity against him in time past.

אֲרוּם תִּנְטְרוּן יַת כָּל תַּפְקֵידְתָּא הֲדָא לְמֶעֱבְדָהּ דַּאֲנָא מְפַקְדְּכוֹן יוֹמָא דֵין לְמִרְחַם יַת יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן וְלִמְהַךְ בְּאָרְחַן דְּתַקְנָן קֳדָמוֹי כָּל יוֹמַיָא וְתוֹסְפוּן לְכוֹן תּוּב תְּלַת קִירְוִין עַל תְּלַת אִילֵין


then shall you keep all this commandment which today I command you to do, that thou mayest love the Lord thy God, and walk in the ways which are right before Him all days; and you shall add yet three cities to those three;

The testimony of one (witness) shall not be valid against a man for any crime (regarding the taking) of life, or guilt concerning money, or any sin with which one may be charged with sinning; but, by the Word of the Lord, (to insure) retribution upon secret crimes, (while) one witness may swear to deny what hath been attested against him, the sentence shall be confirmed upon the mouth of two witnesses, or of three.

## Chapter 20

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:1)


אֲרוּם תִּפְקוּן לְסִדְרֵי קְרָבָא עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן וְתֵיחְמוּן סוּסְוָן וְאַרְתְּכִין עַמִּין גֵּיוְתָנִין וְטֵירוּנִין אַלִּימִין מִנְכוֹן לָא תִדַחֲלוּן מִנְהוֹן אֲרוּם כֻּלְהוֹן חֲשִׁיבִין כְּסוּסְיָא חַד וְכִרְתֵיכָא חַד קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן דְּמֵימְרֵיהּ בְּסַעַדְכוֹן דְּאַפְקְכוֹן פְּרִיקִין מֵאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם


When you go forth to battle against your enemies, and see horses and chariots, and peoples proud, overbearing, and stronger than you, fear them not; for all of them are accounted as a single horse and a single chariot before the Lord your God, whose Word will be your Helper; for He brought you free out of the land of Mizraim.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:2)

And at the time that you draw nigh to do battle, the priest shall approach and speak with the people,

וְיֵימַר לְהוֹן שְׁמָעוּ יִשְרָאֵל אַתּוּן מִתְקַרְבִין יוֹמָא דֵין לְסִדְרֵי קְרָבָא עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן לָא יְזוּחַ לִבְּכוֹן לָא תְדַחֲלוּן וְלָא תִרְתְּחוּן וְלָא תִתְרְעוּן מִן קֳדָמֵיהוֹן


and say to them, Hear, Israel, you draw near this day to fight against your adversaries; let not your heart be moved, be not afraid, tremble not, nor be broken down before them:

אֲרוּם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ מַדְבָּרָא קֳדָמֵיכוֹן לְאַגָחָא מְטוּלְכוֹן עִם בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן לְמִפְרוֹק יַתְכוֹן


for the Shekinah of the Lord your God goeth before you to fight for you against your enemies, and to save you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:5)


וִימַלְלוּן סַרְכַיָא עִם עַמָּא לְמֵימָר מַאן גַּבְרָא דִי בְנָא בֵיתָא חַדְתָּא וְלָא קָבַע בֵּיהּ מְזוּזְתָּא לְשַׁכְלָלוּתֵהּ יְהַךְ וִיתוּב לְבֵיתֵיהּ דִּלְמָא יִגְרוֹם לֵיהּ חוֹבָא וְיִתְקְטֵיל בִּקְרָבָא וּגְבַר חוֹרַן יְשַׁכְלְלִינֵיהּ


And the officers shall speak with the people, saying: Who is the man who hath builded a new house, and hath not set fast its door-posts to complete it? let him go and return to his house, lest through sin he be slain in the battle, and another man complete it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:6)


וּמַאן גַּבְרָא דִּי נְצַב כַּרְמָא וְלָא פַרְקֵיהּ מִן כַּהֲנָא וְאַחֲלֵיהּ יְהַךְ וִיתוּב לְבֵיתֵיהּ דִּלְמָא יִגְרוֹם לֵיהּ חוֹבָא דְלָא פָּרְקֵיהּ וְיִתְקְטֵיל בִּקְרָבָא וּגְבַר חוֹרַן יַחְלִינֵיהּ


Or, what man hath planted a vineyard, and hath not redeemed it from the priest to make it common? let him go and return to his house, lest sin be the occasion of his not redeeming it, but he be slain in the battle, and another make it common.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:7)


וּמַאן גַּבְרָא דְקָדִשׁ אִתְּתָא וְלָא נַסְבָהּ יְהַךְ וִיתוּב לְבֵיתֵיהּ דְּלָא יִגְרוֹם לֵיהּ חוֹבָא דְלָא חָדֵי בְּאִנְתְּתֵיהּ וְיִתְקְטֵיל בִּקְרָבָא וּגְבַר חוֹרַן יַסְבִינָהּ


And what man hath betrothed a wife, but not taken her? let him go and return to his house, lest sin prevent him from rejoicing with his wife and he be slain in the battle, and another take her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:8)


וְיוֹסְפוּן סַרְכַיָא לְמַלָלָא עִם עַמָּא וְיֵימְרוּן מַאן הוּא גַבְרָא דְדָחֵיל מֵחוֹבֵי וְלִבֵּיהּ תְּבִיר יְהַךְ וִיתוּב לְבֵיתֵיהּ דִּלְמָא יְקַבְּלוּן אָחוֹי בְּחוֹבוֹי וְיִתְבַּר לִבְּהוֹן הֵיךְ לִבֵּיהּ


Yet more shall the officers speak to the people, and say, Who is the man who is afraid on account of his sin and whose heart is broken? let him go and return to his house, that his brethren be not implicated in his sins, and their heart be broken like his.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:9)


וִיהֵי כַּד פַּסְקִין סַרְכַיָא לְמַלָּלָא עִם עַמָּא וְיִמְנוּן רַבְרְבָנֵי חֵילִין בְּרֵישׁ עַמָּא


And when the officers shall have finished to speak with the people, they shall appoint the captains of the host at the head of the people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:10)


אֲרוּם תִּקְרְבוּן לְקַרְתָּא לְסַדְרָא עֲלָהּ סִדְרֵי קְרָבָא וּתְשַׁדְּרוּן לְוָותָהּ פּוּלִין לְמִקְרֵי לָהּ לִשְׁלָם


When you come nigh to a city to make war against it, then you shall send to it certain to invite it to peace;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:11)


וִיהֵי אִין מִלִּין דִּשְׁלָם תְּתִיב לְכוֹן וְתִפְתַּח לְכוֹן פּוּלְוָותָהּ וִיהֵי כָּל עַמָּא דְּמִשְׁתַּכַּח בָּהּ יְהוֹן לְמִסְקֵי מִיסִין וְיִפְלְחוּנְכוֹן


and if they answer you with words of peace, and open their gates to you, all the people whom you find therein shall be tributaries, and serve you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:12)


וְאִין לָא תַשְׁלִים עִמְכוֹן וְתַעֲבֵיד עִמְכוֹן קְרָבָא וְתֵקְפוּן עֲלָהּ


But if they will not make peace, but war, with you, then you shall beleaguer it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:13)


וְיִמְסְרִינָהּ יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בִּידֵיכוֹן וְתִמְחֵי יַת כָּל דְּכוּרָהּ לְפִתְגָם דְּחָרֶב


And when the Lord your God will have delivered it into your hand, then may you smite every male thereof with the edge of the sword.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:14)


לְחוֹד נְשַׁיָא וְטַפְלָא וּבְעִירָא וְכָל דִּיהֵי בְקַרְתָּא כָּל עֲדָאָהּ תִּיבְזוּן לְכוֹן וְתֵיכְלוּן יַת עֲדֵי סַנְאֵיכוֹן דִּיהַב יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְכוֹן


But the women, children, and cattle, and whatever is in the city, even all the spoil, you shall seize, and eat the spoil of your enemies which the Lord your God giveth you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:15)


הֵיכְנָא תַּעַבְדוּן לְכָל קִירְוַיָא דִרְחִיקִין מִנְכוֹן לַחֲדָא דְּלָא מִקִרְוֵי שִׁבְעָתֵי עַמְמַיָא הָאִלֵּין הִינוּן


Thus shall you do to all cities that are remote from you, which are not of the cities of these seven nations;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:16)


לְחוֹד מִקִּרְוֵי עַמְמַיָא הָאִילֵן דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן אַחֲסָנָא לָא תְקַיְימוּן כָּל נִשְׁמָא


but of the cities of these peoples, which the Lord your God giveth you to inherit, ye shall not spare alive any breathing thing:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:17)


אֲרוּם גַּמְרָא תְגַמְרִינוּן חִתָּאֵי וֶאֱמוֹרָאֵי כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וּפְרִיזָאֵי וְחִיוָואֵי וִיבוּסָאֵי הֵיכְמָא דְפַקֵּידְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


for destroying ye shall destroy them, Hittites, Amorites, Kenaanites, Pherizites, Hivites, and Jebusites, as the Lord your God hath commanded you;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:18)


מִן בִּגְלַל דְּלָא יַלְפוּן יַתְכוֹן לְמֶעֱבַד כְּכָל מְרַחֲקַתְהוֹן דְּעָבָדוּ לְטַעֲוַותְהוֹן וּתְחוֹבוּן קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


that they may not teach you to do after their abominations with which they have served their idols, and you sin before the Lord your God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:19)


אֲרוּם תַּקְפוּן עַל קַרְתָּא כָּל יוֹמֵי שִׁבְעֲתָא לְאַגָּחָא קְרָבָא עֲלָהּ לְמִכְבְּשָׁהּ בְּשַׁבַּתָּא לָא תְחַבְּלוּן יַת אִילָנַיָהָא לְמֵישְׁדָא עֲלוֹי מָן דְּפַרְזְלָא אֲרוּם מִפֵּירוֹי תֵיכְלוּן וְיָתֵיהּ לָא תְקוּצוּן אֲרוּם לָא בְּבַר נַשׁ אִילָן דְּאַנְפֵּי בָרָא לִמְטַמְרָא מִקָּמֵיכוֹן בִּצְיָירָא


When you beleaguer a city all the seven days to war against it, to subdue it on the Sabbath, you shall not destroy the trees thereof by bringing against them (an instrument of) iron; that you may eat its fruit, cut it not down; for a tree on the face of the field is not as a man to be hidden (put out of sight) before you in the siege.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 20:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/20:20)


לְחוֹד אִילָן דְּתִנְדְּעוּן אֲרוּם לָא אִילָן עֲבִיד פֵּירֵי מֵיכַל הוּא יָתֵיהּ תְּחַבְּלוּן וְתִקְטְעוּן וְתִבְנוּן קַרְקוּמִין עַל קַרְתָּא מַרְדָא דְּעַבְדָא עִמְכוֹן קְרָבָא עַד דְתִכְבְּשׁוּנָהּ


But the tree that you know to be a tree not making fruit to eat, that you may destroy and cut down. And you shall raise bulwarks against the city which maketh war with you, until you have subdued it.



## Chapter 21

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:1)


אֲרוּם יִשְׁתַּכַּח קְטִילָא בְּאַרְעָא דְלָא טָמִיעַ בְּאוּגְרָא בְּאַרְעָא דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהֵיב לְכוֹן לְמֵרְתָהּ רְמֵי וְלָא צְלוּב בְּקִיסָא בְּחַקְלָא וְלָא טָאִיף עַל אַנְפֵּי מַיָא לָא אִשְׁתְּמוֹדַע מַאן קַטְלֵיהּ


If a male be found slain upon the ground, unburied, in the land which the Lord your God giveth you to inherit, lying down, and not hanged on a tree in the field, nor floating on the face of the water; and it be not known who did kill him:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:2)


וְיִפְקוּן מִבֵּי דִינָא רַבָּא תְּרֵין מִן חַכִּימֵיךְ וּתְלַת מִן דַּיָינָךְ וְיִמְשְׁחוּן מֵאַרְבַּע טְרִיגוֹנִין יַת קִרְוַיָא דִּי בְּחַזְרָנוֹת קְטִילָא


then two of the sages shall proceed from the chief court of judgment, and three of thy judges, and shall measure to the surrounding cities which lie on the four quarters from the (spot where) the dead man (is found);


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:3)


וִיהֵי קַרְתָּא דְקָרִיבָא לְקָטִילָא מִתְחַשְׁדָא נַסְבֵי דִינָא רַבָּא מַפְטְרִין וְיִסְבוּן חַכִּימֵי סָבֵי קַרְתָּא הַהִיא עֶגְלַת בְּרַת תּוֹרִין דְּלָא עֵירוֹבִין בְּרַת שַׁתָּא דְּלָא אִתְפְּלַח בָּהּ וְלָא נַגְדַת בְּנִיר


and the city which is nearest to the dead man, being the suspected one, let the chief court of justice take means for absolution (or disculpation). Let the sages, the elders of that city, take an heifer from the herd, not commixed, an heifer of the year, which hath not been wrought with nor hath drawn in the yoke:


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:4)


וְיִחְתוּן חַכִּימֵי קַרְתָּא הַהוּא יַת עִיגְלְתָא לַחֲקַל בַּיַיר דְּלָא יִתְעֲבֵיד בֵּיהּ פּוּלְחַן אֲרַע וְלָא יִזְדְּרַע וְיִנְקְפוּן תַּמָּן יַת עֶגְלָתָא בְּקוֹפִיץ מִבַּתְרָהָא בִּמְצַע חַקְלָא


and the sages of that city shall bring the heifer down into an uncultivated field, where the ground hath not been tilled by work, nor sowed; and let them there behead the heifer from behind her with an axe (or knife, dolch) in the midst of the field.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:5)


וִיקַרְבוּן כַּהֲנַיָא בְּנֵי לֵוִי אֲרוּם בְּהוֹן אִתְרְעֵי מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְשַׁמָשׁוּתֵיהּ וּלְבָרָכָא יַת יִשְרָאֵל בִּשְׁמֵיהּ וְעַל מֵימַר פּוּמְהוֹן יְהִי מִתְדָן כָּל דִּין וְכָל מַכְתַּשׁ צוֹרְעָא לְמִסְגוֹר וּלְמַחְלוֹט


And the priests the sons of Levi shall draw near; for the Lord your God hath chosen them to minister to Him, and to bless Israel in His Name, and according to their words to resolve every judgment, and in any plague of leprosy to shut up, and pronounce concerning it;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:6)


וְכָל חַכִּימֵי קַרְתָּא הַהוּא דְקָרִיבִין לְקָטִילָה יְשַׁזְגוּן יַת יְדֵיהוֹן עַל עֶגְלְתָא דִנְקִיפָא בְּחַקְלָא


and all the elders of the city lying nearest to the dead man shall wash their hands over the heifer which hath been cut off in the field,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:7)


וִיתִיבוּן וְיֵימְרוּן גְלֵי קֳדָם יְיָ דְלָא אָתָא לִידֵינָן וּפַטְרִינֵיה מַן דְשַׁדָא יַת אִידְמָא הָדֵין וְעֵינָנָא לָא חָמוּן


and shall answer and say: It is manifest before the Lord that this hath not come by our hands, nor have we absolved him who shed this blood, nor have our eyes beheld.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:8)


כַּהֲנַיָא יֵימְרוּן כַּפֵּר לְעַמָךְ יִשְרָאֵל דִּפְרַקְתָּא יְיָ וְלָא תְשַׁוֵי חוֹבַת אֲדַם זַכְּאַי בְּגוֹ עַמָּךְ יִשְרָאֵל וּגְלֵי מַן קַטְלֵיהּ וְיִתְכַּפֵּר לְהוֹן עַל דְּמָא וּמִן יַד נַפְקִין נְחִיל דְּמוֹרָנִין מִגוֹ פָּרָתָא דְעֶגְלְתָא נַגְדִין וְאָזְלִין עַד אַתְרָא דְקָטוֹלָא תַמָּן וְסַלְקִין עֲלוֹי וְאַחֲדִין בֵּי דִינָא יָתֵיהּ וְדַיְינִין יָתֵיהּ


And the priests shall say: Let there be expiation for thy people Israel, whom Thou, O Lord, hast redeemed, and lay not the guilt of innocent blood upon Thy people Israel; but let him who hath done the murder be revealed. And they shall be expiated concerning the blood; but straightway there will come forth a swarm of worms from the excrement of the heifer, and spread abroad, and move to. the place where the murderer is, and crawl over him: and the magistrates shall take him, and judge him.

וְאַתּוּן בֵּית יִשְרָאֵל תְּפַלוּן מִשְׁדֵי דַם זַכַּאי מִבֵּינֵיכוֹן אֲרוּם תַּעַבְדוּן דְכָשַׁר קֳדָם יְיָ


So shall you, O house of Israel, put away from among you whosoever sheddeth innocent blood, that you may do what is right before the Lord.

וְתֵיחֲמוּן בִּשְׁבִיתָא אִתָּא שַׁפִּירַת רֵיוֵוי וְתִתְיַדְעוּן בָּהּ וְתִסְבוּנָהּ לְכוֹן לְאִינְתּוּ


if you see in the captivity a woman of fair countenance, and you approve of her, and would take her to you to wife;

וְתַעֲלִינָהּ לְגוֹ בֵּיתָךְ וּתְסַפַּר יַת מַזְיָיא דְרֵישָׁהּ וְתִיצְמֵי יַת טוּפְרְיָיהָא


then thou shalt take her into thy house, and let her cut off the hair of her head, pare her nails,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:13)


וּתְשַׁנֵי יַת כְּסוּת שִׁבְיָיתָהּ מִינָהּ וְתִטְבְּלִינָהּ וּתְגַיְירִינָהּ בְּבֵיתָךְ וְתִבְכֵּי עַל טַעֲוַות בֵּית אַבָּהּ וְאִמָּהּ וְתִשְׁהֵי תְּלַת יַרְחִין דִּי תֵדַע אִין הִיא מְעַבְּרָא וּמִן בָּתַר כְּדֵין תֵּיעוּל לְוָותָהּ וְתַפְרִין יָתָהּ וְתֶהֱוֵי לָךְ לְאִנְתּוּ


and put off the dress of her captivity, and, dipping herself, become a proselyte in thy house, and weep on account of the idols of the house of her father and mother. And thou shalt wait three months to know whether she be with child; and afterwards thou mayest go to her, endow her, and make her thy wife.

וִיהֵי אִין דְּחִיל וּמְקַבֵּל עֲלוֹי אוּלְפָנָא וּבְעַן לְקַיְמוּתֵיהּ מְקַיְימוּנֵיהּ וְאִין תָּאִיב וּמְרִיד יְאַטְלוּנֵיהּ כָּל אַנְשֵׁי קַרְתֵּיהּ בְּאַבְנַיָא וִימוּת וּתְפַלּוּן עֲבִיד דְּבִישׁ מִבֵּינֵיכוֹן וְכָל יִשְרָאֵל יִשְׁמְעוּן וִידַחֲלוּן


And it shall be that if he brought to fear and receive instruction, and beg that his life may be spared, you shall let him live; but if he refuse and continue rebellious, then all the men of his city shall stone him with stones that he die; and so shall you put away the evil doer from among you, and all Israel will hear, and be afraid.

When a man hath become guilty of the judgment of death, and is condemned to be stoned, and they afterwards hang him on a beam,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 21:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/21:23)


לָא תָבִית נִבְלַת גּוּשְׁמֵיהּ עַל קֵיסָא אֲרוּם מִקְבַּר תִּקְבְּרוּנֵיהּ בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא אֲרוּם קִילוּתָא קֳדָם אֱלָהָא לְמִצְלוֹב גְּבַר אֱלָהֵן חוֹבוֹי גָרְמוּ לֵיהּ וּמִן בִּגְלַל דִּבְדִיוּקְנָא דַיְיָ אִתְעֲבֵד תְּקַבְּרוּנֵיהּ עִם מִטְמוֹעַ שִׁמְשָׁא דְּלָא יְקִילִין בִּרְיָיתָא בֵּיהּ וְלָא תְטוּפוּן בִּנְבֵילְתְּהוֹן דְּחַיָּיבָא יַת אַרְעֲכוֹן דַּיְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן יָהִיב לְכוֹן אַחֲסָנָא


his dead body shall not remain upon the beam, but he shall be certainly buried on the same day; for it is execrable before God to hang a man, but that his guilt gave occasion for it; and because he was made in the image of God, you shall bury him at the going down of the sun, lest wild beasts abuse him, and lest you overspread your land, which the Lord your God giveth you to possess, with the dead bodies of criminals.



## Chapter 22

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:1)


לָא תֶחְמוּן יַת תּוֹרָא דַּאֲחוּכוֹן אוֹ יַת אִמְרֵיהּ טַעֲיָין וּתְפַלְגוּן דַּעְתְּכוֹן מִנְהוֹן אָתָבָא תְּתִיבִינוּן לֵיהּ


Thou shalt not see thy brother’s ox or his lamb going astray, and estrange thy knowledge from them; thou shalt certainly restore them to him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:2)


וְאִין לָא קְרִיבָא דַעְתָּא דְאָחוּךְ עִמָךְ אוֹ לָא חַכִּימְתֵּיהּ וְתִכְנְשִׁינֵיהּ לְגוֹ בֵיתָךְ וִיהֵי מִתְפַּרְנֵס גַּבָּךְ עַד זְמַן דְּיִתְבַּע אָחוּךְ יָתֵיהּ וּתְתִיבִינֵיהּ לֵיהּ


But if knowledge of thy brother is not thine, if thou knowest him not, thou shalt bring it into thy house, and it shall be supported by thee till the time that thou hast sought out thy brother, and thou shalt restore it to him.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:3)


וְהֵיכְדֵין תַּעֲבֵיד לְחַמְרֵיהּ וְהֵיכְדֵין תַּעֲבֵיד לִכְסוּתֵיהּ וְהֵיכְדֵין תַּעֲבֵיד לְכָל אֲבֵידְתָּא דְּאָחוּךְ דִּי מִתְבְּדָא מִנֵּיהּ וְתַשְׁכְּחָהּ לֵית לָךְ רְשׁוּ לְכַסָּאָהּ מִינֵיהּ אַכְרִיז עֲלָהּ וּתְהַדְרִינָהּ


So shalt thou do with his ass, with his garment, and with any lost thing of thy brother’s. If thou find, it is not lawful for thee to hide it from him; thou shalt cry it, and restore it.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:4)


לָא תֶחְמוּן יַת חֲמָרָא דַאֲחוּכוֹן אוֹ תוֹרֵי רְמָאָן בְּאוֹרְחָא וְתִכְבְּשׁוּן עֵינֵיכוֹן מִנְהוֹן מֵיקַם תְּקוּמוּן עִמֵּיהּ

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:5)


לָא יִהְיֶה גּוּלְיַין דְּצִיצִית וּתְפִילִין דְּהִינוּן תִּקּוּנֵי גְבַר עַל אִיתָּא וְלָא יְסַפֵּר גְּבַר בֵּי שֵׁיחַיַא וְעִרְיְיתֵיהּ וּבֵי אַנְפּוֹי לְאִיתְחֲמָאָה הֵיךְ נְשָׁא אֲרוּם מְרַחֵק קֳדָם יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן הוּא כָּל דְּעָבִיד אִלֵּין


Neither fringed robes nor tephillin which are the ornaments of a man shall be upon a woman; neither shall a man shave himself so as to appear like a woman; for every one who doeth so is an abomination before the Lord thy God.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:6)


אֲרוּם אִין אִזְדַמָּן שַׁרְכְּפָא דְּצִיפַּר דְּכֵי קַמָּךְ בְּאִסְרְטָא בְּכָל אִילָן אוֹ עַל אַרְעָא גּוֹזְלִין אוֹ בַּעֲיַין וְאִימָא רְבִיעָא עַל אֶפְרוֹחִין אוֹ עִילוֹי בַּעֲיַין לָא תִסַּב אִימָא מֵעַל בְּנַיָא


If thou find the nest of a clean bird before thee in the way, in a tree, or upon the ground, in which there are young ones or eggs, and the mother sitting upon the young ones or eggs,

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:7)


מִפְטַר תִּפְטוֹר יַת אִמָּא וְיַת בְּנַיָא תִסַּב לָךְ מִן בִּגְלַל דְּיוֹטַב לָךְ בְּעַלְמָא הָדֵין וְתוֹרִיךְ יוֹמִין בְּעַלְמָא דְאָתֵי


thou shalt be sure to send the mother away, but thou mayest take the young for thyself that it may be well with thee in this world, and that thou mayest prolong tby days in the world to come.

אֲרוּם תִּבְנֶה בֵּיתָא חַדְתָּא וְתַעֲבֵיד תֵּיאַק גִּפוּפִין לְאִיגְרָךְ לָא תִגְרְמוּן לְאִסְתַּקְפָא חוֹבַת אֲדַם דִּיקְטוֹל בְּבֵיתָךְ דִּלְמָא יִפּוֹל דֵּין דְּחָמֵי לְמֵיפַל מִנֵּיהּ

לָא תִזְרְעוּן כַּרְמֵיכוֹן עֵירוּבִין דִּלְמָא תִתְחַיֵּיב יַקִידְתָּא דִּימְעַת זַרְעָא דְתִזְרְעוּן וַעֲלָלַת כַּרְמָא

Even if she be with child, they shall not wait till she is delivered, but in the same hour they shall put them to death by strangulation with the napkin, and cast away the evil doer from Israel.

אֲרוּם בְּאַנְפֵּי בָּרָא אַשְׁכְּחָהּ פָּגְנַת עוּלֵּימְתָּא דְמִיקַדְשָׁא וְלָא אִזְדַמֵּן פַּרִיק לָהּ


he found her upon the face of the field; the betrothed damsel cried out for help, but there was no one to deliver her.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 22:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:28)


אֲרוּם יַשְׁכַּח גְּבַר עוּלֵימְתָּא בְּתוּלְתָּא דְלָא מִיקַדְשָׁא וְיֵחוּד בָּהּ וּמְשַׁמֵּשׁ עִמָּהּ וּמִשְׁתַּכְּחִין


If a man find a damsel who is not betrothed, and seize and lie with her, and they be found,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 22:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/22:29)


וְיִתֵּן גַּבְרָא דְשַׁמֵּשׁ עִמָּהּ לְאָבוּהָא דְעוּלֵּימְתָּא דְמֵי בַּהֲתָהּ חַמְשִׁין סַלְעִין דִּכְסַף וְלֵיהּ תְּהֵי לְאִינְתּוּ חוֹלַף דְּעַנְיָיהּ לֵית לֵיהּ רְשׁוּ לְמִפְטְרָהּ בְּגִיטָא כָּל יוֹמוֹי


then the man who lay with her shall give to her father, as a fine for her dishonour, fifty shekels of silver, and she shall be his wife, because he humbled her, nor shall he have power to put her away by divorcement all his days.



## Chapter 23

###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:1)


לָא יִסַּב גְּבַר יַת אִתְּתָא דְּאָנִיס אוֹ דְשַׁרְגִיג אָבוֹי וְכָל דְּכֵן אִתַּת אָבוֹי וְלָא יְגַלֵי כַנְפָא דְגַלֵּי אָבוֹי


A man should not take a wife who is bowed down (or violated), or who hath had intercourse with his father, much less his father’s wife, nor disclose the skirt that covereth his father.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:2)


לָא יִדְכֵּי דִמְסָרֵיס וּדְפָסִיק גִידָא לְמִיסַב אִיתָא מִקְהַל עַמָּא דַיְיָ


He who is castrated is not fit to take a wife from the congregation of the Lord’s people.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:3)


לָא יִדְכֵּי דְמִתְיְלִיד מִן זְנוּ דְבֵיהּ מוּמָא בִּישָׁא דְאִיתְיְהִיב בְּעַמְמַיָא חוֹלוֹנָאֵי לְמֵיסַב אִיתָא כַּשְׁרָא מִקְהַל עַמָּא דַיְיָ אוֹף דּוֹר עֲשִירָאֵי לָא יִדְכֵּי לֵיהּ לְמֵיעַל בִּקְהָלָא דַיְיָ


He who is born of fornication, or who hath upon him the evil mark which is set upon the unclean Gentiles, is not fit to take an upright wife from the congregation of the people of the Lord; nor unto the tenth generation shall it be fit for him to enter into the congregation of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:4)


לָא יִדְכּוּן דְּכוּרֵי עַמּוֹנָאֵי וּמוֹאֲבָאֵי לְמֵיסַב אִיתָא מִקְהַל עַמָּא דַיְיָ בְּרַם דַּר עֲשִירָאֵי לָא יִדְכֵּי לְמֵיסַב אִיתָא מִקְהַל עַמָּא דַיְיָ עַד עָלְמָא


Neither an Ammonite nor a Moabite man is fit to take a wife from the congregation of the Lord’s people, nor unto the tenth generation shall they take a wife from the congregation of the people of the Lord,


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:5)


עַל עֵיסַק דְּלָא זְמִינוּ לְכוֹן בְּלַחֲמָא וּבְמוֹי בְּאָרְחָא בְּמִפַּקְכוֹן מִמִּצְרַיִם וּדְאוֹגַר לְכוֹן יַת בִּלְעָם בַּר בְּעוֹר מִן פְּתוֹר חֶלְמַיָא דְּמִתְבַּנְיָא בְּאַרַע אֲרַם דְּעַל פְּרָת לְמֵילַט יַתְכוֹן


because they met you not with bread and water in the way when you came from Mizraim, but hired against you Bileam bar Beor from Petho Chelmaya, which is built in the land of Aram upon the Phrat, to curse you;


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:6)


וְלָא צָבָא יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן לְקַבָּלָא מִן בִּלְעָם וְהָפַךְ יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן בְּפוּמֵיהּ מְטוֹלְכוֹן יַת לְוָוטִין לְבִרְכַן אֲרוּם רָחֵמִינְכוֹן יְיָ אֱלָהָכוֹן


but the Lord your God would not hearken unto Bileam, but turned in his mouth curses into blessings, because the Lord your God loveth you.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:7)


לָא תִתְבְּעוּן שְׁלָמְהוֹן וְטָבְהוֹן כָּל יוֹמֵיכוֹן דַּאֲפִלּוּ מִתְגַיְירִין סְנָא נְטִיר בְּלִבְּהוֹן עַד עַלְמָא


Ye shall not seek their peace or, their prosperity all your days, because, if even they become proselytes, they will entertain enmity in their hearts for ever.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:8)


לָא תְרַחֲקוּן אֱדוֹמָאָה דְאָתֵי לְאִתְגַיְירָא אֲרוּם אֲחוּכוֹן הוּא לָא תְרַחֲקִין מִצְרָאָה דְּאָתֵי לְאִתְגַיְירָא אֲרוּם דַּיְירוּן הֲוֵיתוּן בְּאַרְעֲהוֹן


You shall not abhor an Edomite when he cometh to be a proselyte, for he is your brother; nor shall you abhor a Mizraite, because you were dwellers in their land.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:9)


בְּנִין דְּמִתְיַלְדִין לְהוֹן דַּר תְּלִיתָאָה יִדְכּוּן לְמִסְבָּא מֵעַם קְהָלָה דַיְיָ


The children who are born to them in the third generation shalt be fit to take wives from the people of the congregation of the Lord.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:10)


אֲרוּם תִּפְקוֹן מַשִׁירְוַיָן עַל בַּעֲלֵי דְבָבֵיכוֹן וְתִסְתַּמְּרוּן מִכָּל פִּתְגָם דְּבִישׁ מִפּוּלְחָנָא נוּכְרַיָא וְגִילוּי עֶרְיְיתָא וְשַׁדְיוּת אֲדַם זַכַּאי


When you go forth in hosts against your enemies, beware of every evil thing, of strange worship, the, exposure of the shame, and the shedding of innocent blood.


###### Targum Jonathan on Deuteronomy 23:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Targum%20Jonathan%20on%20Deuteronomy/r/23:11)


אֲרוּם יְהֵי בָךְ גְּבַר דְּלָא יְהֵי דְכֵי מִקִּרְיוּת הִרְהוּר לֵילְיָא וְיִפּוֹק לְמִבָּרָא לְמַשְׁרִיתָא לָא יֵיעוֹל לְמִצּוּעַ מַשְׁרִיתָא


Should there be a man among you who is unclean from accidents of the night, let him go without the camp, and come not among the tents.
